## **🔔 SEKSI 1: IKHTISAR WEBHOOK & EVENT SYSTEM 1MIN.AI**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **Webhook & Event System** untuk:
1. **Notifikasi Real-Time** – Terima *callback* saat tugas AI selesai (misal: generasi video, gambar, atau transkripsi).
2. **Event-Driven Workflow** – Pemicu alur kerja otomatis berdasarkan *event* (misal: `task.completed`, `asset.uploaded`).
3. **Integrasi dengan Sistem Eksternal** – Kirim data ke *third-party services* (Slack, Discord, database, dll.).

**Kasus Penggunaan:**
- Notifikasi saat video selesai diproses.
- Pemicu *downstream processing* (misal: unggah video ke YouTube setelah generasi).
- Logging dan monitoring tugas AI.

---
---
---
## **🎣 SEKSI 2: WEBHOOK OVERVIEW**
---

### **2.1 Konsep Webhook**
- **Webhook** adalah *HTTP callback* yang dikirim oleh **1Min.AI** ke *endpoint* Anda saat *event* tertentu terjadi.
- **Event** yang didukung:
  - `task.completed` – Tugas AI selesai (misal: generasi gambar/video, transkripsi).
  - `task.failed` – Tugas AI gagal.
  - `asset.uploaded` – File berhasil diunggah.
  - `asset.deleted` – File dihapus.
  - `conversation.created` – Percakapan baru dibuat.
  - `conversation.updated` – Percakapan diperbarui.

---
### **2.2 Alur Kerja Webhook**
```
1. Daftarkan endpoint webhook Anda di dashboard 1Min.AI.
2. 1Min.AI mengirim POST request ke endpoint Anda saat event terjadi.
3. Endpoint Anda memproses data dan merespons dengan HTTP 200.
```

**Contoh Alur:**
1. Anda meminta generasi video via `IMAGE_TO_VIDEO`.
2. **1Min.AI** memproses permintaan (memakan waktu beberapa detik/menit).
3. Saat video selesai, **1Min.AI** mengirim *webhook* ke endpoint Anda dengan detail:
   - `taskId`
   - `status` (`completed`)
   - `videoUrl`
   - `creditsUsed`

---
### **2.3 Daftarkan Webhook**
**Cara Daftar:**
1. Buka [Dashboard 1Min.AI](https://app.1min.ai).
2. Navigasi ke **Settings → Webhooks**.
3. Klik **Add Webhook**.
4. Isi:
   - **Endpoint URL** (misal: `https://musgo-os.example.com/webhooks/1min-ai`).
   - **Events** yang ingin didengarkan (misal: `task.completed`, `task.failed`).
   - **Secret Key** (opsional, untuk verifikasi *signature*).

**Contoh Form Daftar Webhook:**
| Field          | Value                          | Keterangan                                  |
|----------------|--------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| Endpoint URL   | `https://your-server.com/webhook` | URL endpoint webhook Anda.                 |
| Events         | `task.completed`, `task.failed` | Pilih event yang ingin didengarkan.        |
| Secret Key     | `your_secret_key`              | Kunci rahasia untuk verifikasi *signature*.|

---
---
---
## **🌐 SEKSI 3: ENDPOINT WEBHOOK & PAYLOAD**
---

### **3.1 Endpoint Webhook**
**Endpoint Anda** harus:
- Menerima **POST request** dari **1Min.AI**.
- Mengembalikan **HTTP 200** dalam waktu **5 detik** (jika tidak, 1Min.AI akan *retry*).
- Memvalidasi *signature* (jika menggunakan *Secret Key*).

**Contoh Endpoint (Python + FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, HTTPException
import hmac
import hashlib

app = FastAPI()
SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"  # Sama dengan yang didaftarkan di 1Min.AI

@app.post("/webhooks/1min-ai")
async def handle_webhook(request: Request):
    # 1. Baca body dan header
    body = await request.body()
    signature = request.headers.get("X-1Min-Signature")

    # 2. Validasi signature (jika menggunakan Secret Key)
    if SECRET_KEY:
        expected_signature = hmac.new(
            SECRET_KEY.encode(),
            body,
            hashlib.sha256
        ).hexdigest()
        if signature != expected_signature:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Invalid signature")

    # 3. Parse payload
    payload = await request.json()

    # 4. Proses event
    event_type = payload.get("event")
    if event_type == "task.completed":
        await handle_task_completed(payload)
    elif event_type == "task.failed":
        await handle_task_failed(payload)
    elif event_type == "asset.uploaded":
        await handle_asset_uploaded(payload)
    else:
        print(f"Unhandled event: {event_type}")

    # 5. Respon dengan 200
    return {"status": "success"}

async def handle_task_completed(payload: dict):
    task_id = payload["taskId"]
    video_url = payload.get("videoUrl")
    print(f"Task {task_id} completed! Video URL: {video_url}")
    # Simpan ke database, kirim notifikasi, dll.

async def handle_task_failed(payload: dict):
    task_id = payload["taskId"]
    error = payload.get("error")
    print(f"Task {task_id} failed: {error}")

async def handle_asset_uploaded(payload: dict):
    asset_id = payload["assetId"]
    asset_url = payload["assetUrl"]
    print(f"Asset {asset_id} uploaded! URL: {asset_url}")
```

---
### **3.2 Payload Webhook**
**Struktur Payload Umum:**
```json
{
  "event": "task.completed",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z",
  "data": {
    "taskId": "task_abc123",
    "status": "completed",
    "type": "IMAGE_TO_VIDEO",
    "model": "kling",
    "videoUrl": "https://assets.1min.ai/videos/output.mp4",
    "creditsUsed": 20,
    "duration": 5,
    "createdAt": "2026-05-19T11:59:00Z",
    "completedAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

**Payload untuk Setiap Event:**
| Event               | Payload Contoh                                                                                     |
|---------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `task.completed`    | `{"event": "task.completed", "data": {"taskId": "...", "status": "completed", "videoUrl": "..."}}` |
| `task.failed`       | `{"event": "task.failed", "data": {"taskId": "...", "status": "failed", "error": "..."}}`         |
| `asset.uploaded`    | `{"event": "asset.uploaded", "data": {"assetId": "...", "assetUrl": "...", "type": "image"}}`      |
| `asset.deleted`     | `{"event": "asset.deleted", "data": {"assetId": "...", "deletedAt": "..."}}`                      |
| `conversation.created` | `{"event": "conversation.created", "data": {"conversationId": "...", "userId": "..."}}`       |
| `conversation.updated` | `{"event": "conversation.updated", "data": {"conversationId": "...", "newMessage": "..."}}`   |

---
### **3.3 Header Webhook**
**1Min.AI** mengirim header berikut dengan setiap *webhook*:
| Header               | Deskripsi                                                                                     |
|----------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `X-1Min-Event`       | Tipe event (misal: `task.completed`).                                                       |
| `X-1Min-Signature`   | *HMAC-SHA256 signature* dari body (jika *Secret Key* dikonfigurasi).                         |
| `X-1Min-Timestamp`   | Timestamp saat event dikirim (ISO 8601).                                                     |
| `X-1Min-Request-Id`  | ID unik untuk permintaan webhook (untuk debugging).                                          |

---
---
---
## **🔌 SEKSI 4: INTEGRASI WEBHOOK DENGAN MUSGO-OS 2IN1**
---
---

### **4.1 Arsitektur Integrasi**
**MusGo-OS 2in1** dapat mengintegrasikan **Webhook 1Min.AI** untuk:
1. **Modul Notifikasi:**
   - Kirim notifikasi ke pengguna saat tugas selesai.
2. **Modul Pemrosesan Otomatis:**
   - Pemicu alur kerja *downstream* (misal: unggah video ke CDN).
3. **Modul Monitoring:**
   - Log semua event untuk *audit trail*.

**Contoh Arsitektur:**
```
[1Min.AI] → (Webhook POST) → [MusGo-OS 2in1 Webhook Handler] → [Database/Notifikasi/Alur Kerja]
```

---
### **4.2 Contoh Implementasi (Python + FastAPI)**
**1. Setup Webhook Handler:**
```python
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, HTTPException, BackgroundTasks
import hmac
import hashlib
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI()
SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"
DB_FILE = "musgo_webhooks.db"

# Setup database
conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS webhook_events (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        event_type TEXT,
        task_id TEXT,
        asset_id TEXT,
        conversation_id TEXT,
        payload TEXT,
        timestamp TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/webhooks/1min-ai")
async def handle_webhook(request: Request, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks):
    # 1. Validasi signature
    body = await request.body()
    signature = request.headers.get("X-1Min-Signature")
    if SECRET_KEY:
        expected_signature = hmac.new(
            SECRET_KEY.encode(),
            body,
            hashlib.sha256
        ).hexdigest()
        if signature != expected_signature:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Invalid signature")

    # 2. Parse payload
    payload = await request.json()
    event_type = payload.get("event")
    data = payload.get("data", {})

    # 3. Simpan ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO webhook_events (event_type, task_id, asset_id, conversation_id, payload, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (
            event_type,
            data.get("taskId"),
            data.get("assetId"),
            data.get("conversationId"),
            str(payload)
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    # 4. Proses event di background
    background_tasks.add_task(process_webhook_event, payload)

    return {"status": "success"}

async def process_webhook_event(payload: dict):
    event_type = payload["event"]
    data = payload["data"]

    if event_type == "task.completed":
        await handle_task_completed(data)
    elif event_type == "task.failed":
        await handle_task_failed(data)
    elif event_type == "asset.uploaded":
        await handle_asset_uploaded(data)
    elif event_type == "conversation.created":
        await handle_conversation_created(data)
```

**2. Handler untuk Setiap Event:**
```python
from fastapi import BackgroundTasks
import requests

async def handle_task_completed(data: dict):
    task_id = data["taskId"]
    video_url = data.get("videoUrl")
    user_id = data.get("userId")  # Jika tersedia

    # 1. Simpan ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO completed_tasks (task_id, video_url, user_id, completed_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (task_id, video_url, user_id)
    )
    conn.commit()

    # 2. Kirim notifikasi (misal: Slack)
    if user_id:
        slack_webhook_url = "https://hooks.slack.com/services/..."
        message = {
            "text": f"✅ Tugas AI selesai! Task ID: {task_id}\nVideo URL: {video_url}"
        }
        requests.post(slack_webhook_url, json=message)

    # 3. Pemicu alur kerja downstream (misal: unggah ke CDN)
    if video_url:
        await upload_to_cdn(video_url)

async def handle_task_failed(data: dict):
    task_id = data["taskId"]
    error = data.get("error")

    # 1. Log kesalahan
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO failed_tasks (task_id, error, failed_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (task_id, error)
    )
    conn.commit()

    # 2. Kirim notifikasi kesalahan
    slack_webhook_url = "https://hooks.slack.com/services/..."
    message = {
        "text": f"❌ Tugas AI gagal! Task ID: {task_id}\nError: {error}"
    }
    requests.post(slack_webhook_url, json=message)

async def handle_asset_uploaded(data: dict):
    asset_id = data["assetId"]
    asset_url = data["assetUrl"]
    asset_type = data["type"]

    # Simpan ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO uploaded_assets (asset_id, asset_url, type, uploaded_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (asset_id, asset_url, asset_type)
    )
    conn.commit()

async def handle_conversation_created(data: dict):
    conversation_id = data["conversationId"]
    user_id = data["userId"]

    # Simpan ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO conversations (conversation_id, user_id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (conversation_id, user_id)
    )
    conn.commit()

async def upload_to_cdn(video_url: str):
    # Contoh: Unggah ke Cloudflare R2
    cdn_endpoint = "https://your-cdn.example.com/upload"
    response = requests.post(cdn_endpoint, json={"url": video_url})
    if response.status_code == 200:
        print(f"Video uploaded to CDN: {video_url}")
    else:
        print(f"Failed to upload to CDN: {response.text}")
```

---
### **4.3 Modul Keamanan Tambahan**
Untuk memastikan integrasi **secure-by-default** dan **zero-trust compliant**, tambahkan:

#### **1. Verifikasi Signature**
- **Selalu validasi `X-1Min-Signature`** untuk menghindari *spoofing*.
- Contoh (Python):
  ```python
  def verify_signature(body: bytes, signature: str, secret_key: str) -> bool:
      expected_signature = hmac.new(
          secret_key.encode(),
          body,
          hashlib.sha256
      ).hexdigest()
      return hmac.compare_digest(expected_signature, signature)
  ```

#### **2. Rate Limiting untuk Webhook**
- Batasi jumlah *webhook requests* per menit untuk menghindari *DDoS*.
- Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):
  ```python
  from slowapi import Limiter
  from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

  limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
  app.state.limiter = limiter

  @app.post("/webhooks/1min-ai")
  @limiter.limit("100/minute")
  async def handle_webhook(request: Request):
      ...
  ```

#### **3. IP Whitelisting**
- Hanya terima *webhook* dari **IP 1Min.AI**.
- Daftar IP 1Min.AI:
  - `52.200.0.0/16` (AWS US-West-2)
  - `3.100.0.0/16` (AWS US-East-1)
  - `18.200.0.0/16` (AWS EU-West-1)

**Contoh (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException

ALLOWED_IPS = {"52.200.0.0/16", "3.100.0.0/16", "18.200.0.0/16"}

def is_allowed_ip(client_ip: str) -> bool:
    for ip_range in ALLOWED_IPS:
        if client_ip.startswith(ip_range.rsplit(".", 1)[0]):
            return True
    return False

@app.post("/webhooks/1min-ai")
async def handle_webhook(request: Request):
    client_ip = request.client.host
    if not is_allowed_ip(client_ip):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="IP not allowed")
    ...
```

#### **4. Retry & Idempotency**
- **1Min.AI** akan *retry* webhook jika tidak menerima **HTTP 200**.
- Gunakan **idempotency key** untuk menghindari pemrosesan ganda.

**Contoh (Python):**
```python
# Setup tabel untuk idempotency
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS webhook_idempotency (
        idempotency_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        processed_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")

async def handle_webhook(request: Request):
    # 1. Baca idempotency key dari header
    idempotency_key = request.headers.get("X-1Min-Request-Id")
    if not idempotency_key:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing idempotency key")

    # 2. Cek apakah sudah diproses
    cursor.execute("SELECT 1 FROM webhook_idempotency WHERE idempotency_key = ?", (idempotency_key,))
    if cursor.fetchone():
        return {"status": "already processed"}

    # 3. Proses webhook
    payload = await request.json()
    # ... (proses payload)

    # 4. Simpan idempotency key
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO webhook_idempotency (idempotency_key, processed_at) VALUES (?, datetime('now'))",
        (idempotency_key,)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success"}
```

## **📡 SEKSI 5: EVENT SYSTEM & POLLING (ALTERNATIF WEBHOOK)**
---
Jika **webhook** tidak memungkinkan (misal: *firewall*, *no public endpoint*), **1Min.AI** juga menyediakan **Polling** untuk memeriksa status tugas.

---

### **5.1 Polling untuk Status Tugas**
**Endpoint:**
```
GET https://api.1min.ai/api/tasks/{taskId}
```
**Header:**
```http
API-KEY: <api-key>
```

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/tasks/task_abc123' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "completed",
  "taskId": "task_abc123",
  "type": "IMAGE_TO_VIDEO",
  "result": {
    "videoUrl": "https://assets.1min.ai/videos/output.mp4",
    "duration": 5,
    "creditsUsed": 20
  },
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T11:59:00Z",
  "completedAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---
### **5.2 Implementasi Polling (Python)**
```python
import requests
import time

def poll_task_status(task_id: str, api_key: str, max_attempts: int = 30, interval: int = 5):
    url = f"https://api.1min.ai/api/tasks/{task_id}"
    headers = {"API-KEY": api_key}

    for attempt in range(max_attempts):
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
        if response.status_code != 200:
            raise Exception(f"Failed to poll task: {response.text}")

        data = response.json()
        status = data["status"]

        if status == "completed":
            return data["result"]
        elif status == "failed":
            raise Exception(f"Task failed: {data.get('error')}")
        elif status in ["processing", "queued"]:
            print(f"Task {task_id} is {status}. Attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts}")
            time.sleep(interval)
        else:
            raise Exception(f"Unknown status: {status}")

    raise Exception(f"Task {task_id} did not complete after {max_attempts} attempts")

# Contoh penggunaan
try:
    result = poll_task_status("task_abc123", "YOUR_API_KEY")
    print(f"Video URL: {result['videoUrl']}")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"Error: {e}")
```

---
### **5.3 Perbandingan Webhook vs Polling**
| Fitur               | Webhook                          | Polling                          |
|---------------------|----------------------------------|----------------------------------|
| **Real-Time**       | ✅ Ya                            | ❌ Tidak (delay tergantung *interval*) |
| **Kemudahan**       | ❌ Membutuhkan *public endpoint* | ✅ Tidak memerlukan *public endpoint* |
| **Skalabilitas**    | ✅ Baik untuk *high volume*      | ❌ Kurang efisien untuk *high volume* |
| **Keandalan**       | ❌ Bergantung pada *network*      | ✅ Lebih andal (klien kontrol *retry*) |
| **Biaya**           | ✅ Tidak ada *outbound traffic*  | ❌ Ada *outbound traffic* (klien menanyakan) |

## **🔒 SEKSI 7: KEAMANAN & COMPLIANCE UNTUK WEBHOOK**
---
---

### **7.1 Zero Trust untuk Webhook**
- **Verifikasi Semua Permintaan:**
  - Validasi `X-1Min-Signature`.
  - Validasi `X-1Min-Request-Id` (idempotency).
  - Validasi IP asal.

**Contoh Middleware (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException
import hmac
import hashlib

SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"
ALLOWED_IPS = {"52.200.0.0/16", "3.100.0.0/16"}

@app.middleware("http")
async def verify_webhook(request: Request, call_next):
    # 1. Cek IP
    client_ip = request.client.host
    if not any(client_ip.startswith(ip.rsplit(".", 1)[0]) for ip in ALLOWED_IPS):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="IP not allowed")

    # 2. Cek signature (jika ada)
    if request.url.path == "/webhooks/1min-ai":
        body = await request.body()
        signature = request.headers.get("X-1Min-Signature")
        if SECRET_KEY and signature:
            expected_signature = hmac.new(
                SECRET_KEY.encode(),
                body,
                hashlib.sha256
            ).hexdigest()
            if not hmac.compare_digest(expected_signature, signature):
                raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Invalid signature")

    return await call_next(request)
```

---
### **7.2 Immutable Logging untuk Webhook**
- **Simpan Semua Event ke IPFS/Blockchain:**
  - Gunakan **IPFS** untuk *tamper-proof logging*.

**Contoh (Python + IPFS):**
```python
import ipfshttpclient
from datetime import datetime

client = ipfshttpclient.connect()

def log_webhook_event(payload: dict):
    log_entry = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "event": payload["event"],
        "data": payload["data"],
        "signature": payload.get("signature")
    }
    log_hash = client.add_json(log_entry)
    return f"ipfs://{log_hash}"
```

---
### **7.3 Rate Limiting & DDoS Protection**
- **Batasi Permintaan Webhook:**
  - Gunakan *rate limiting* untuk menghindari *DDoS*.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/webhooks/1min-ai")
@limiter.limit("100/minute")
async def handle_webhook(request: Request):
    ...
```

---
### **7.4 Enkripsi Data Sensitif**
- **Enkripsi Payload Webhook:**
  - Jika payload mengandung data sensitif, enkripsi sebelum penyimpanan.

**Contoh (Python + Fernet):**
```python
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

key = Fernet.generate_key()
cipher_suite = Fernet(key)

def encrypt_payload(payload: dict) -> str:
    return cipher_suite.encrypt(str(payload).encode()).decode()

def decrypt_payload(encrypted_payload: str) -> dict:
    return eval(cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_payload.encode()).decode())
```

  - **Rate Limiting & DDoS Protection**.

## **🧠 SEKSI 1: IKHTISAR AI MEMORY & BRAND VOICE**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **AI Memory** dan **Brand Voice** untuk:
1. **AI Memory** – Mempertahankan **konteks percakapan** lintas sesi, memungkinkan AI untuk "mengingat" informasi dari interaksi sebelumnya.
2. **Brand Voice** – Menyesuaikan *tone*, *style*, dan *personality* respons AI agar konsisten dengan merek atau kebutuhan pengguna.

**Kasus Penggunaan:**
- **Chatbot dengan Memory:** AI mengingat preferensi pengguna (misal: nama, riwayat pembelian).
- **Asisten Virtual:** AI mempertahankan konteks proyek atau tugas.
- **Brand Consistency:** Respons AI selalu mengikuti *brand voice* (misal: formal, kasual, teknis).

---
---
---
## **🔄 SEKSI 2: AI MEMORY (KONTEKS PERCAKAPAN PERSISTEN)**
---

### **2.1 Konsep AI Memory**
- **AI Memory** memungkinkan AI untuk:
  - **Mengingat konteks** dari percakapan sebelumnya.
  - **Mengakses informasi historis** (misal: riwayat pesan, preferensi pengguna).
  - **Membangun respons yang lebih relevan** berdasarkan *memory* yang tersimpan.

**Contoh:**
- Pengguna: *"Apa status proyek yang kita bahas kemarin?"*
- AI (dengan Memory): *"Kemarin kita membahas proyek X. Statusnya saat ini adalah 70% selesai."*

---
### **2.2 Cara Kerja AI Memory**
1. **Aktifkan Memory:**
   - Gunakan parameter `conversationId` yang **sama** untuk semua pesan dalam satu percakapan.
   - **1Min.AI** secara otomatis menyimpan konteks percakapan.
2. **Simpan Memory Eksternal:**
   - Gunakan **Conversation History API** untuk menyimpan dan mengambil *memory* dari database eksternal.
3. **Sinkronisasi Memory:**
   - Secara berkala sinkronkan *memory* dari **1Min.AI** ke sistem lokal (misal: database, vector store).

---
### **2.3 Parameter AI Memory**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Contoh                          |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------|
| `conversationId`        | string  | ID unik untuk percakapan. Gunakan ID yang sama untuk mempertahankan konteks.               | `"conv_abc123"`                |
| `memory`                | object  | Objek *memory* yang berisi konteks tambahan (opsional).                                       | `{"user_preferences": {...}}` |
| `remember`              | boolean | Instruksi untuk AI untuk "mengingat" informasi ini untuk percakapan selanjutnya.             | `true`                          |
| `forget`                | boolean | Instruksi untuk AI untuk "melupakan" informasi ini.                                          | `false`                         |

---
### **2.4 Contoh Penggunaan AI Memory**
**Contoh 1: Percakapan dengan Memory (Python)**
```python
import requests

# Mulai percakapan
response = requests.post(
    "https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai",
    headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
    json={
        "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
        "model": "gpt-4o",
        "promptObject": {
            "prompt": "Halo, nama saya Dhani. Saya suka AI dan cybersecurity.",
            "conversationId": "conv_abc123",  # ID yang sama untuk semua pesan
            "remember": True  # Instruksi AI untuk mengingat
        }
    }
)
print(response.json()["response"])

# Lanjutkan percakapan (AI mengingat nama dan preferensi)
response = requests.post(
    "https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai",
    headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
    json={
        "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
        "model": "gpt-4o",
        "promptObject": {
            "prompt": "Apa yang saya suka?",
            "conversationId": "conv_abc123"  # ID yang sama
        }
    }
)
print(response.json()["response"])  # Output: "Anda suka AI dan cybersecurity, Dhani."
```

**Contoh 2: Menyimpan Memory Eksternal (Database)**
```python
import sqlite3

# Setup database
conn = sqlite3.connect("ai_memory.db")
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversation_memory (
        conversation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        user_id TEXT,
        memory TEXT,
        updated_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")

# Simpan memory ke database
def save_memory(conversation_id: str, user_id: str, memory: dict):
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO conversation_memory (conversation_id, user_id, memory, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (conversation_id, user_id, str(memory))
    )
    conn.commit()

# Ambil memory dari database
def load_memory(conversation_id: str):
    cursor.execute("SELECT memory FROM conversation_memory WHERE conversation_id = ?", (conversation_id,))
    result = cursor.fetchone()
    return eval(result[0]) if result else {}

# Contoh penggunaan
memory = {"user_name": "Dhani", "interests": ["AI", "cybersecurity"]}
save_memory("conv_abc123", "user_123", memory)

# Ambil memory untuk percakapan
loaded_memory = load_memory("conv_abc123")
print(loaded_memory)  # Output: {'user_name': 'Dhani', 'interests': ['AI', 'cybersecurity']}
```

---
### **2.5 Sinkronisasi Memory dengan 1Min.AI**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **Conversation History API** untuk sinkronisasi *memory*:
1. **Ambil History Percakapan:**
   ```python
   response = requests.get(
       "https://api.1min.ai/api/conversations/conv_abc123",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
   )
   history = response.json()["messages"]
   ```
2. **Simpan ke Database Lokal:**
   ```python
   for message in history:
       cursor.execute(
           "INSERT INTO chat_history (conversation_id, role, content, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
           (message["conversationId"], message["role"], message["content"], message["timestamp"])
       )
   conn.commit()
   ```
3. **Gunakan Memory untuk Respons AI:**
   ```python
   # Ambil history percakapan
   cursor.execute("SELECT content FROM chat_history WHERE conversation_id = ? ORDER BY timestamp", ("conv_abc123",))
   history = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]

   # Kirim ke AI dengan memory
   response = requests.post(
       "https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
           "model": "gpt-4o",
           "promptObject": {
               "prompt": "Apa yang kita bahas sebelumnya?",
               "conversationId": "conv_abc123",
               "memory": {"history": history}  # Sertakan history sebagai memory
           }
       }
   )
   ```

---
### **2.6 Praktik Terbaik untuk AI Memory**
1. **Gunakan `conversationId` yang Konsisten:**
   - Selalu gunakan ID yang sama untuk satu percakapan.
2. **Batasi Ukuran Memory:**
   - Hindari menyimpan terlalu banyak konteks (bisa menghabiskan *token* dan mempengaruhi performa).
   - Contoh: Batasi *memory* hingga **10 pesan terakhir**.
3. **Sinkronisasi Berkala:**
   - Secara berkala sinkronkan *memory* dari **1Min.AI** ke database lokal.
4. **Enkripsi Data Sensitif:**
   - Enkripsi *memory* yang mengandung data sensitif (misal: PII).
5. **Hapus Memory Lama:**
   - Hapus *memory* yang sudah tidak aktif (misal: > 30 hari).

**Contoh Pembersihan Memory:**
```python
def cleanup_old_memory(days: int = 30):
    cursor.execute(
        "DELETE FROM conversation_memory WHERE updated_at < datetime('now', ?)",
        (f"-{days} days",)
    )
    conn.commit()
```

## **🎭 SEKSI 3: BRAND VOICE (PENYESUAIAN TONE & STYLE)**
---

### **3.1 Konsep Brand Voice**
- **Brand Voice** memungkinkan Anda untuk menyesuaikan *tone*, *style*, dan *personality* respons AI agar konsisten dengan merek atau kebutuhan.
- **Contoh Brand Voice:**
  - **Formal:** Respons profesional, sopan, dan struktur.
  - **Casual:** Respons santai, ramah, dan informal.
  - **Technical:** Respons dengan terminologi teknis dan detail.
  - **Creative:** Respons kreatif, imajinatif, dan inovatif.

---
### **3.2 Parameter Brand Voice**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Contoh                          |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------|
| `brandVoice`            | string  | Tipe *brand voice* yang diinginkan.                                                          | `"formal"`, `"casual"`, `"technical"` |
| `tone`                  | string  | *Tone* respons (opsional).                                                                   | `"professional"`, `"friendly"` |
| `style`                 | string  | *Style* penulisan (opsional).                                                                | `"concise"`, `"detailed"`       |
| `personality`           | string  | *Personality* AI (opsional).                                                                | `"helpful"`, `"humorous"`       |
| `customInstructions`   | string  | Instruksi kustom untuk AI (opsional).                                                       | `"Gunakan bahasa Indonesia formal."` |

---
### **3.3 Daftar Brand Voice yang Didukung**
| Brand Voice       | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Contoh Penggunaan                          |
|-------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------|
| `formal`          | Respons profesional, sopan, dan struktur.                                                   | Chatbot untuk layanan pelanggan.          |
| `casual`          | Respons santai, ramah, dan informal.                                                        | Asisten virtual untuk pengguna muda.      |
| `technical`       | Respons dengan terminologi teknis dan detail.                                              | Dokumentasi API atau dukungan teknis.     |
| `creative`        | Respons kreatif, imajinatif, dan inovatif.                                                   | Pembuatan konten kreatif.                 |
| `concise`         | Respons singkat dan langsung ke inti.                                                       | Ringkasan atau jawaban cepat.             |
| `detailed`        | Respons terperinci dan komprehensif.                                                        | Penjelasan mendalam atau tutorial.        |
| `custom`          | *Brand voice* kustom yang didefinisikan oleh pengguna.                                       | Sesuai dengan kebutuhan merek.            |

---
### **3.4 Contoh Penggunaan Brand Voice**
**Contoh 1: Brand Voice Formal (Python)**
```python
response = requests.post(
    "https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai",
    headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
    json={
        "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
        "model": "gpt-4o",
        "promptObject": {
            "prompt": "Tulis email untuk klien tentang penawaran proyek.",
            "brandVoice": "formal",
            "tone": "professional",
            "style": "detailed"
        }
    }
)
print(response.json()["response"])
```

**Contoh 2: Brand Voice Custom (cURL)**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
  "model": "gpt-4o",
  "promptObject": {
    "prompt": "Jelaskan tentang MusGo-OS 2in1.",
    "brandVoice": "custom",
    "customInstructions": "Gunakan bahasa Indonesia formal. Sertakan detail teknis tentang keamanan dan skalabilitas."
  }
}'
```

**Contoh 3: Brand Voice untuk Code Generation**
```python
response = requests.post(
    "https://api.1min.ai/api/features",
    headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
    json={
        "type": "CODE_GENERATOR",
        "model": "qwen3-coder-plus",
        "promptObject": {
            "prompt": "Buat fungsi Python untuk validasi input.",
            "brandVoice": "technical",
            "style": "concise"
        }
    }
)
print(response.json()["response"])
```

---
### **3.5 Praktik Terbaik untuk Brand Voice**
1. **Definisikan Brand Voice yang Jelas:**
   - Tentukan *tone*, *style*, dan *personality* yang konsisten dengan merek.
2. **Gunakan `customInstructions` untuk Detail:**
   - Berikan instruksi spesifik untuk AI (misal: *"Gunakan bahasa Indonesia formal"*).
3. **Uji Brand Voice:**
   - Uji respons AI dengan berbagai *brand voice* untuk memastikan konsistensi.
4. **Simpan Brand Voice di Database:**
   - Simpan *brand voice* untuk setiap pengguna atau proyek.

**Contoh Database Brand Voice:**
```python
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS brand_voices (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id TEXT,
        project_id TEXT,
        brand_voice TEXT,
        tone TEXT,
        style TEXT,
        personality TEXT,
        custom_instructions TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")

def save_brand_voice(user_id: str, project_id: str, brand_voice: dict):
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO brand_voices (user_id, project_id, brand_voice, tone, style, personality, custom_instructions, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (
            user_id,
            project_id,
            brand_voice.get("brandVoice"),
            brand_voice.get("tone"),
            brand_voice.get("style"),
            brand_voice.get("personality"),
            brand_voice.get("customInstructions")
        )
    )
    conn.commit()
```

## **🔌 SEKSI 4: INTEGRASI AI MEMORY & BRAND VOICE DENGAN MUSGO-OS 2IN1**
---
---

### **4.1 Arsitektur Integrasi**
**MusGo-OS 2in1** dapat mengintegrasikan **AI Memory** dan **Brand Voice** untuk:
1. **Modul Chat AI:**
   - Gunakan `conversationId` untuk mempertahankan konteks.
   - Terapkan *brand voice* yang konsisten.
2. **Modul AI Assistant:**
   - Simpan *memory* percakapan untuk referensi masa depan.
   - Sesuaikan respons dengan *brand voice* pengguna.
3. **Modul Analisis:**
   - Gunakan *memory* untuk analisis tren atau pola percakapan.

**Contoh Arsitektur:**
```
[User] → [MusGo-OS 2in1] → (API Gateway) → [1Min.AI Chat API] → (AI Memory + Brand Voice)
[MusGo-OS 2in1] → [Database Lokal] → (Simpan Memory & Brand Voice)
```

---
### **4.2 Contoh Implementasi (Python + FastAPI)**
**1. Setup Endpoint untuk Chat dengan Memory & Brand Voice:**
```python
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
import requests
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI()
DB_FILE = "musgo_memory.db"

# Setup database
conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
        conversation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        user_id TEXT,
        model TEXT,
        brand_voice TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversation_memory (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        conversation_id TEXT,
        role TEXT,
        content TEXT,
        timestamp TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY (conversation_id) REFERENCES conversations(conversation_id)
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/chat")
async def chat(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    prompt = data["prompt"]
    conversation_id = data.get("conversationId")
    brand_voice = data.get("brandVoice", "formal")

    # 1. Buat conversationId jika belum ada
    if not conversation_id:
        conversation_id = f"conv_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
        cursor.execute(
            "INSERT INTO conversations (conversation_id, user_id, model, brand_voice, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
            (conversation_id, user_id, "gpt-4o", brand_voice)
        )
        conn.commit()

    # 2. Ambil memory percakapan
    cursor.execute(
        "SELECT role, content FROM conversation_memory WHERE conversation_id = ? ORDER BY timestamp",
        (conversation_id,)
    )
    memory = [{"role": row[0], "content": row[1]} for row in cursor.fetchall()]

    # 3. Kirim ke 1Min.AI dengan memory dan brand voice
    response = requests.post(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "promptObject": {
                "prompt": prompt,
                "conversationId": conversation_id,
                "memory": memory,
                "brandVoice": brand_voice
            }
        }
    )

    # 4. Simpan respons ke memory
    ai_response = response.json()["response"]
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO conversation_memory (conversation_id, role, content, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (conversation_id, "assistant", ai_response)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {
        "conversationId": conversation_id,
        "response": ai_response,
        "brandVoice": brand_voice
    }
```

**2. Setup Endpoint untuk Mengelola Brand Voice:**
```python
@app.post("/brand-voice")
async def set_brand_voice(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    project_id = data.get("projectId")
    brand_voice = {
        "brandVoice": data.get("brandVoice", "formal"),
        "tone": data.get("tone"),
        "style": data.get("style"),
        "personality": data.get("personality"),
        "customInstructions": data.get("customInstructions")
    }

    # Simpan brand voice ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO brand_voices (user_id, project_id, brand_voice, tone, style, personality, custom_instructions, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (
            user_id,
            project_id,
            brand_voice["brandVoice"],
            brand_voice.get("tone"),
            brand_voice.get("style"),
            brand_voice.get("personality"),
            brand_voice.get("customInstructions")
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "brandVoice": brand_voice}

@app.get("/brand-voice/{user_id}")
async def get_brand_voice(user_id: str):
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM brand_voices WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,))
    brand_voices = []
    for row in cursor.fetchall():
        brand_voices.append({
            "id": row[0],
            "projectId": row[2],
            "brandVoice": {
                "brandVoice": row[3],
                "tone": row[4],
                "style": row[5],
                "personality": row[6],
                "customInstructions": row[7]
            }
        })
    return {"brandVoices": brand_voices}
```

---
### **4.3 Modul Keamanan Tambahan**
Untuk memastikan integrasi **secure-by-default** dan **zero-trust compliant**, tambahkan:

#### **1. Enkripsi Memory**
- Enkripsi *memory* yang mengandung data sensitif.

**Contoh (Python + Fernet):**
```python
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

key = Fernet.generate_key()
cipher_suite = Fernet(key)

def encrypt_memory(memory: dict) -> str:
    return cipher_suite.encrypt(str(memory).encode()).decode()

def decrypt_memory(encrypted_memory: str) -> dict:
    return eval(cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_memory.encode()).decode())
```

#### **2. Validasi Brand Voice**
- Validasi *brand voice* yang dikirim oleh pengguna.

**Contoh:**
```python
ALLOWED_BRAND_VOICES = {"formal", "casual", "technical", "creative", "concise", "detailed", "custom"}

def validate_brand_voice(brand_voice: dict) -> bool:
    if brand_voice.get("brandVoice") not in ALLOWED_BRAND_VOICES:
        return False
    return True
```

#### **3. Rate Limiting untuk Chat**
- Batasi jumlah permintaan chat per pengguna.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/chat")
@limiter.limit("100/minute")
async def chat(request: Request):
    ...
```

#### **4. Audit Logging**
- Catat semua aktivitas chat dan *brand voice*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import logging

logging.basicConfig(filename='ai_memory.log', level=logging.INFO)

def log_chat_activity(user_id: str, conversation_id: str, prompt: str, response: str):
    logging.info(
        f"{datetime.now()} | User: {user_id} | Conversation: {conversation_id} | Prompt: {prompt[:50]}... | Response: {response[:50]}..."
    )
```

## **🔒 SEKSI 6: KEAMANAN & COMPLIANCE UNTUK AI MEMORY & BRAND VOICE**
---
---

### **6.1 Zero Trust untuk AI Memory**
- **Verifikasi Semua Akses:**
  - Pastikan hanya pengguna yang berwenang yang dapat mengakses *memory* percakapan.
- **Enkripsi Data:**
  - Enkripsi *memory* yang mengandung data sensitif.

**Contoh Middleware (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException
import jwt

SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"

async def verify_user(request: Request):
    token = request.headers.get("Authorization")
    if not token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing token")

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token.split(" ")[1], SECRET_KEY, algorithms=["HS256"])
        return payload["user_id"]
    except:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token")

@app.post("/chat")
async def chat(request: Request):
    user_id = await verify_user(request)
    data = await request.json()
    if data["userId"] != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Unauthorized")
    ...
```

---
### **6.2 Replay Protection untuk Memory**
- **Gunakan Nonce:**
  - Tambahkan `nonce` untuk setiap permintaan *memory* untuk menghindari *replay attack*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import secrets

def generate_nonce() -> str:
    return secrets.token_hex(16)

@app.post("/chat")
async def chat(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    nonce = data.get("nonce")
    if not nonce or not validate_nonce(nonce):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid nonce")
    ...
```

---
### **6.3 Immutable Logging untuk AI Memory**
- **Simpan Semua Aktivitas ke IPFS:**
  - Gunakan **IPFS** untuk *tamper-proof logging* aktivitas *memory* dan *brand voice*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import ipfshttpclient

client = ipfshttpclient.connect()

def log_memory_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    log_entry = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "user_id": user_id,
        "action": action,
        "details": details
    }
    log_hash = client.add_json(log_entry)
    return f"ipfs://{log_hash}"
```

---
### **6.4 Compliance (GDPR, PDP)**
- **Hapus Data Lama:**
  - Hapus *memory* yang sudah tidak aktif (misal: > 30 hari).
- **Anonimisasi Data:**
  - Anonimisasi data sensitif dalam *memory*.

**Contoh:**
```python
def anonymize_memory(memory: dict) -> dict:
    if "user_name" in memory:
        memory["user_name"] = "*****"
    if "email" in memory:
        memory["email"] = "*****@*****.com"
    return memory
```

## **🤝 SEKSI 1: IKHTISAR AI SOCIAL & COLLABORATION FEATURES**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **AI Social & Collaboration Features** untuk:
1. **AI Social** – Platform kolaborasi di mana pengguna dapat **berbagi, mendiskusikan, dan berkolaborasi** dalam pembuatan konten AI (gambar, video, teks, dll.).
2. **Multi-AI Chat** – Berinteraksi dengan **beberapa model AI sekaligus** dalam satu percakapan.
3. **Referral System** – Sistem *referral* untuk mendapatkan **kredit gratis** dengan mengundang teman.
4. **Leaderboard** – *Leaderboard* untuk melihat **kontributor teratas** dan mendapatkan hadiah.
5. **Team Collaboration** – Kolaborasi tim dengan **ruang kerja bersama** dan *permission management*.

**Kasus Penggunaan:**
- **Komunitas AI:** Pengguna berbagi kreasi AI (gambar, video, kode) dan saling memberikan *feedback*.
- **Kolaborasi Tim:** Tim bekerja bersama dalam proyek AI dengan *role* dan *permission* yang jelas.
- **Monetisasi:** Pengguna mendapatkan *reward* (kredit, hadiah) melalui *referral* dan kontribusi.

---
---
---
## **🌍 SEKSI 2: AI SOCIAL (PLATFORM KOLABORASI AI)**
---

### **2.1 Konsep AI Social**
**AI Social** adalah fitur **1Min.AI** yang memungkinkan pengguna untuk:
- **Berbagi konten AI** (gambar, video, teks, kode) ke *feed* publik atau grup privat.
- **Mendapatkan *feedback*** dari komunitas.
- **Mengikuti pengguna lain** dan melihat kreasi mereka.
- **Mendiskusikan topik AI** dalam *thread* atau *chat grup*.

**Contoh:**
- Pengguna A mengunggah gambar yang dibuat dengan **DALL·E 3**.
- Pengguna B memberikan *like* dan *comment* pada gambar tersebut.
- Pengguna C *share* gambar ke *feed*-nya dengan kredit ke pengguna A.

---
### **2.2 Endpoint & Autentikasi**
**Base URL:**
```
https://api.1min.ai/api/social
```
**Header Wajib:**
| Field        | Value              | Keterangan                          |
|--------------|--------------------|------------------------------------|
| `API-KEY`    | `<your_api_key>`   | Kunci API dari dashboard 1Min.AI   |
| `Authorization` | `Bearer <user_token>` | Token pengguna (untuk autentikasi user-level). |

---
### **2.3 Endpoint & Metode AI Social**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/posts`                     | `POST` | Buat *post* baru.                          | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts"` |
| `/posts`                     | `GET`  | Daftar *post* (dengan filter).             | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts?userId=user_123"` |
| `/posts/{postId}`            | `GET`  | Ambil detail *post*.                      | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/post_abc123"` |
| `/posts/{postId}`            | `DELETE`| Hapus *post*.                             | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/post_abc123"` |
| `/posts/{postId}/like`       | `POST` | *Like* sebuah *post*.                     | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/post_abc123/like"` |
| `/posts/{postId}/comments`   | `POST` | Tambah *comment* ke *post*.               | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/post_abc123/comments"` |
| `/posts/{postId}/comments`   | `GET`  | Daftar *comment* dari *post*.              | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/post_abc123/comments"` |
| `/users/{userId}/follow`     | `POST` | *Follow* pengguna.                        | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/users/user_123/follow"` |
| `/users/{userId}/unfollow`   | `POST` | *Unfollow* pengguna.                      | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/users/user_123/unfollow"` |
| `/users/{userId}/posts`      | `GET`  | Daftar *post* dari pengguna.              | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/users/user_123/posts"` |
| `/feed`                      | `GET`  | Daftar *post* dari *feed* pengguna.       | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/feed"` |

---
### **2.4 Buat Post (Create Post)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `title`                 | string  | Judul *post*.                                                                               | ✅     |
| `content`               | string  | Konten *post* (teks).                                                                         | ❌     |
| `assetUrl`              | string  | URL asset (gambar, video, audio) yang sudah diunggah via **Asset API**.                     | ❌     |
| `assetType`             | string  | Tipe asset (`image`, `video`, `audio`, `document`).                                          | ❌     |
| `tags`                  | array   | *Tags* untuk *post*.                                                                         | ❌     |
| `visibility`            | string  | Visibilitas (`public`, `private`, `group`).                                                 | ❌     |
| `groupId`               | string  | ID grup (jika `visibility=group`).                                                           | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer <user_token>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "title": "Gambar Cyberpunk dengan DALL·E 3",
  "content": "Ini adalah gambar kota cyberpunk yang saya buat dengan DALL·E 3. Bagaimana menurut kalian?",
  "assetUrl": "images/cyberpunk_city.jpg",
  "assetType": "image",
  "tags": ["cyberpunk", "AI", "DALL·E 3"],
  "visibility": "public"
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "postId": "post_abc123",
  "title": "Gambar Cyberpunk dengan DALL·E 3",
  "content": "Ini adalah gambar kota cyberpunk yang saya buat dengan DALL·E 3. Bagaimana menurut kalian?",
  "assetUrl": "images/cyberpunk_city.jpg",
  "assetType": "image",
  "tags": ["cyberpunk", "AI", "DALL·E 3"],
  "visibility": "public",
  "authorId": "user_123",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z",
  "updatedAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---

## **🤖 SEKSI 3: MULTI-AI CHAT (INTERAKSI DENGAN BEBERAPA MODEL AI)**
---

### **3.1 Konsep Multi-AI Chat**
**Multi-AI Chat** memungkinkan pengguna untuk:
- **Berinteraksi dengan beberapa model AI sekaligus** dalam satu percakapan.
- **Membandingkan respons** dari berbagai model.
- **Menggabungkan keunggulan** setiap model (misal: `GPT-4o` untuk *reasoning*, `Claude-4-6-Sonnet` untuk *coding*).

**Contoh:**
- Pengguna: *"Jelaskan tentang Zero Trust Architecture dan berikan contoh kode Python."*
- **Multi-AI Chat** akan:
  1. Mengirim pertanyaan ke **GPT-4o** (untuk penjelasan).
  2. Mengirim pertanyaan ke **Claude-4-6-Sonnet** (untuk kode).
  3. Menggabungkan respons dari kedua model.

---
### **3.2 Endpoint Multi-AI Chat**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai
```
**Parameter (`promptObject`):**
| Parameter       | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-----------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `prompt`        | string  | Pertanyaan atau *prompt*.                                                                     | ✅     |
| `models`        | array   | Daftar model AI yang akan digunakan.                                                        | ✅     |
| `conversationId`| string  | ID percakapan (opsional).                                                                     | ❌     |
| `combine`       | boolean | Jika `true`, gabungkan respons dari semua model. Jika `false`, kembalikan respons terpisah. | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/chat-with-ai' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "type": "CHAT_WITH_AI",
  "promptObject": {
    "prompt": "Jelaskan tentang Zero Trust Architecture dan berikan contoh kode Python.",
    "models": ["gpt-4o", "claude-4-6-sonnet"],
    "combine": true
  }
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan (Combine = true):**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "conversationId": "conv_multi_123",
  "response": {
    "explanation": "Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA) adalah model keamanan yang berasumsi bahwa setiap akses ke sistem harus divalidasi...",
    "code_example": "class ZeroTrustValidator:\n    def validate_access(self, user, resource):\n        # Implementasi validasi\n        pass"
  },
  "modelsUsed": ["gpt-4o", "claude-4-6-sonnet"],
  "creditsUsed": 35
}
```

**Contoh Tanggapan (Combine = false):**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "conversationId": "conv_multi_123",
  "responses": [
    {
      "model": "gpt-4o",
      "response": "Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA) adalah model keamanan yang berasumsi bahwa setiap akses ke sistem harus divalidasi...",
      "creditsUsed": 20
    },
    {
      "model": "claude-4-6-sonnet",
      "response": "class ZeroTrustValidator:\n    def validate_access(self, user, resource):\n        # Implementasi validasi\n        pass",
      "creditsUsed": 15
    }
  ],
  "totalCreditsUsed": 35
}
```

---
### **3.3 Praktik Terbaik untuk Multi-AI Chat**
1. **Pilih Model dengan Bijak:**
   - Gunakan model yang **spesialis** untuk tugas tertentu (misal: `Claude-4-6-Sonnet` untuk *coding*, `GPT-4o` untuk *reasoning*).
2. **Batasi Jumlah Model:**
   - Hindari menggunakan terlalu banyak model sekaligus (bisa menghabiskan kredit dengan cepat).
3. **Gunakan `combine` untuk Efisiensi:**
   - Jika memungkinkan, gunakan `combine: true` untuk menghemat *round-trip* API.
4. **Simpan Respons Terpisah:**
   - Jika `combine: false`, simpan respons dari setiap model untuk analisis lebih lanjut.

## **🎁 SEKSI 4: REFERRAL SYSTEM (SISTEM RUJUKAN)**
---

### **4.1 Konsep Referral System**
**Referral System** memungkinkan pengguna untuk:
- **Mendapatkan kredit gratis** dengan mengundang teman.
- **Melacak *referral*** (siapa yang diundang, kapan, berapa kredit yang didapat).
- **Mendapatkan *reward*** tambahan untuk *top referrers*.

**Contoh:**
- Pengguna A mengundang Pengguna B via *referral link*.
- Pengguna B mendaftar dan menggunakan layanan **1Min.AI**.
- Pengguna A mendapatkan **100 kredit gratis**.

---
### **4.2 Endpoint Referral System**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/referrals`                 | `POST` | Buat *referral link*.                      | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals"` |
| `/referrals`                 | `GET`  | Daftar *referral* pengguna.                | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals"` |
| `/referrals/{referralId}`    | `GET`  | Ambil detail *referral*.                  | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals/ref_abc123"` |
| `/referrals/claim`           | `POST` | Klaim *referral reward*.                  | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals/claim"` |

---
### **4.3 Buat Referral Link**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter | Tipe   | Deskripsi                          | Wajib? |
|-----------|--------|------------------------------------|--------|
| `userId`  | string | ID pengguna yang membuat *referral*. | ✅     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer <user_token>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "userId": "user_123"
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "referralId": "ref_abc123",
  "referralLink": "https://1min.ai/ref/user_123",
  "userId": "user_123",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z",
  "expiresAt": "2026-06-19T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---
### **4.4 Daftar Referral**
**Endpoint:**
```
GET https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals
```
**Parameter Query Opsional:**
| Parameter | Tipe    | Deskripsi                          | Contoh               |
|-----------|---------|------------------------------------|----------------------|
| `userId`  | string  | Filter by user ID.                 | `userId=user_123`    |
| `status`  | string  | Filter by status (`pending`, `claimed`, `expired`). | `status=claimed` |
| `limit`   | number  | Batasan jumlah *referral*.         | `limit=10`          |
| `page`    | number  | Halaman (untuk paginasi).          | `page=1`            |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals?userId=user_123&status=claimed' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer <user_token>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "referrals": [
    {
      "referralId": "ref_abc123",
      "referralLink": "https://1min.ai/ref/user_123",
      "userId": "user_123",
      "referredUserId": "user_456",
      "status": "claimed",
      "creditsEarned": 100,
      "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z",
      "claimedAt": "2026-05-19T12:05:00Z"
    },
    {
      "referralId": "ref_def456",
      "referralLink": "https://1min.ai/ref/user_123",
      "userId": "user_123",
      "referredUserId": "user_789",
      "status": "pending",
      "creditsEarned": 100,
      "createdAt": "2026-05-18T10:30:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "total": 2,
  "page": 1,
  "limit": 10
}
```

### **4.5 Klaim Referral Reward**

```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/referrals/claim
```

## **🏆 SEKSI 5: LEADERBOARD (PAPAN PERINGKAT)**
---

### **5.1 Konsep Leaderboard**
**Leaderboard** menampilkan:
- **Top Kontributor** – Pengguna dengan *referral* terbanyak.
- **Top Creators** – Pengguna dengan *post* atau *konten* terbanyak.
- **Top Engagers** – Pengguna dengan *like* dan *comment* terbanyak.
- **Reward System** – Hadiah untuk pengguna teratas (misal: kredit tambahan, akses premium).

---
### **5.2 Endpoint Leaderboard**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/leaderboard`               | `GET`  | Daftar *leaderboard* (default: referrals). | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/leaderboard"` |
| `/leaderboard/referrals`     | `GET`  | Daftar *leaderboard* untuk *referrals*.    | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/leaderboard/referrals"` |
| `/leaderboard/posts`         | `GET`  | Daftar *leaderboard* untuk *post*.        | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/leaderboard/posts"` |
| `/leaderboard/engagement`    | `GET`  | Daftar *leaderboard* untuk *engagement*.  | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/leaderboard/engagement"` |

---

```
GET https://api.1min.ai/api/leaderboard
```

| Parameter | Tipe    | Deskripsi                          | Contoh               |
|-----------|---------|------------------------------------|----------------------|
| `type`    | string  | Tipe *leaderboard* (`referrals`, `posts`, `engagement`). | `type=referrals` |
| `limit`   | number  | Batasan jumlah entri.              | `limit=10`          |
| `page`    | number  | Halaman (untuk paginasi).          | `page=1`            |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**

## **👥 SEKSI 6: TEAM COLLABORATION (KOLABORASI TIM)**
---

### **6.1 Konsep Team Collaboration**
**Team Collaboration** memungkinkan:
- **Ruang Kerja Bersama** – Tim dapat berkolaborasi dalam proyek AI.
- **Permission Management** – Atur *role* dan *permission* untuk setiap anggota tim.
- **Shared Assets** – Berbagi *asset* (gambar, video, dokumen) antar anggota tim.
- **Shared Credits** – Berbagi *kredit* antar anggota tim (opsional).

---
### **6.2 Endpoint Team Collaboration**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/teams`                     | `POST` | Buat tim baru.                             | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams"` |
| `/teams/{teamId}`            | `GET`  | Ambil detail tim.                         | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/team_abc123"` |
| `/teams/{teamId}/members`    | `POST` | Tambah anggota tim.                       | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/team_abc123/members"` |
| `/teams/{teamId}/members/{userId}` | `DELETE` | Hapus anggota tim.               | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/team_abc123/members/user_123"` |
| `/teams/{teamId}/assets`     | `GET`  | Daftar *asset* tim.                        | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/team_abc123/assets"` |
| `/teams/{teamId}/credits`    | `GET`  | Lihat *kredit* tim.                        | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/team_abc123/credits"` |

---
### **6.3 Buat Tim (Create Team)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/teams
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter | Tipe   | Deskripsi                          | Wajib? |
|-----------|--------|------------------------------------|--------|
| `name`    | string | Nama tim.                          | ✅     |
| `description` | string | Deskripsi tim (opsional).      | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/teams' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer <user_token>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "name": "Tim AI Panji Alam Clan",
  "description": "Tim pengembangan AI untuk proyek MusGo-OS 2in1."
}'
```

```json
  "ownerId": "user_123",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---
### **6.6 Permission Management**
**Role & Permission:**
| Role      | Deskripsi                                                                                     |
|-----------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `owner`   | Pemilik tim. Dapat mengelola anggota, asset, dan kredit.                                    |
| `admin`   | Admin tim. Dapat mengelola anggota dan asset, tetapi tidak dapat menghapus tim.             |
| `member`  | Anggota tim. Dapat mengunggah asset dan menggunakan kredit tim.                             |
| `viewer`  | Penonton. Hanya dapat melihat asset dan aktivitas tim (tidak dapat mengunggah).            |

**Contoh Aturan Permission:**
- **Owner/Admin:**
  - Tambah/hapus anggota.
  - Kelola asset tim.
  - Kelola kredit tim.
- **Member:**
  - Unggah asset.
  - Gunakan kredit tim (jika diizinkan).
- **Viewer:**
  - Lihat asset dan aktivitas tim.

## **🔌 SEKSI 7: INTEGRASI AI SOCIAL & COLLABORATION DENGAN MUSGO-OS 2IN1**
---
---

### **7.1 Arsitektur Integrasi**
**MusGo-OS 2in1** dapat mengintegrasikan **AI Social & Collaboration Features** untuk:
1. **Modul Komunitas:**
   - Buat *feed* internal untuk berbagi konten AI.
   - Tambahkan *like* dan *comment* untuk kolaborasi.
2. **Modul Tim:**
   - Kelola tim dan *permission* untuk proyek AI.
   - Berbagi *asset* dan *kredit* antar anggota tim.
3. **Modul Referral:**
   - Integrasikan *referral system* untuk mendapatkan kredit tambahan.
   - Pantau *leaderboard* untuk motivasi tim.

**Contoh Arsitektur:**
```
[User] → [MusGo-OS 2in1] → (API Gateway) → [1Min.AI AI Social API] → (Database Lokal)
[User] → [MusGo-OS 2in1] → (API Gateway) → [1Min.AI Team API] → (Database Lokal)
```

---
### **7.2 Contoh Implementasi (Python + FastAPI)**
**1. Setup Endpoint untuk AI Social:**
```python
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, HTTPException
import requests
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI()
DB_FILE = "musgo_social.db"

# Setup database
conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (
        post_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        user_id TEXT,
        title TEXT,
        content TEXT,
        asset_url TEXT,
        asset_type TEXT,
        tags TEXT,
        visibility TEXT,
        like_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        comment_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        created_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS comments (
        comment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        post_id TEXT,
        user_id TEXT,
        content TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY (post_id) REFERENCES posts(post_id)
    )
""")
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS likes (
        post_id TEXT,
        user_id TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        PRIMARY KEY (post_id, user_id),
        FOREIGN KEY (post_id) REFERENCES posts(post_id)
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/social/posts")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    title = data["title"]
    content = data.get("content", "")
    asset_url = data.get("assetUrl")
    asset_type = data.get("assetType")
    tags = data.get("tags", [])
    visibility = data.get("visibility", "public")

    # 1. Unggah asset ke 1Min.AI (jika ada)
    if asset_url and asset_type:
        # Asumsikan asset sudah diunggah via Asset API
        pass

    # 2. Buat post di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Authorization": f"Bearer {data['userToken']}"},
        json={
            "title": title,
            "content": content,
            "assetUrl": asset_url,
            "assetType": asset_type,
            "tags": tags,
            "visibility": visibility
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to create post")

    post_data = response.json()

    # 3. Simpan post ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO posts (post_id, user_id, title, content, asset_url, asset_type, tags, visibility, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (
            post_data["postId"],
            user_id,
            title,
            content,
            asset_url,
            asset_type,
            ",".join(tags),
            visibility
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "postId": post_data["postId"]}

@app.post("/social/posts/{post_id}/like")
async def like_post(post_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]

    # 1. Like post di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/{post_id}/like",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Authorization": f"Bearer {data['userToken']}"}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to like post")

    # 2. Simpan like ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO likes (post_id, user_id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (post_id, user_id)
    )
    cursor.execute(
        "UPDATE posts SET like_count = like_count + 1 WHERE post_id = ?",
        (post_id,)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "likeCount": response.json()["likeCount"]}

@app.post("/social/posts/{post_id}/comments")
async def add_comment(post_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    content = data["content"]

    # 1. Tambah comment di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/social/posts/{post_id}/comments",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Authorization": f"Bearer {data['userToken']}"},
        json={"content": content}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to add comment")

    comment_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan comment ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO comments (comment_id, post_id, user_id, content, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (comment_data["commentId"], post_id, user_id, content)
    )
    cursor.execute(
        "UPDATE posts SET comment_count = comment_count + 1 WHERE post_id = ?",
        (post_id,)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "commentId": comment_data["commentId"]}
```

**2. Setup Endpoint untuk Team Collaboration:**
```python
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS teams (
        team_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT,
        description TEXT,
        owner_id TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS team_members (
        team_id TEXT,
        user_id TEXT,
        role TEXT,
        joined_at TIMESTAMP,
        PRIMARY KEY (team_id, user_id),
        FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams(team_id)
    )
""")
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS team_assets (
        asset_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        team_id TEXT,
        asset_url TEXT,
        type TEXT,
        uploaded_by TEXT,
        uploaded_at TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams(team_id)
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/teams")
async def create_team(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    name = data["name"]
    description = data.get("description", "")

    # 1. Buat tim di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/teams",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Authorization": f"Bearer {data['userToken']}"},
        json={"name": name, "description": description}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to create team")

    team_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan tim ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO teams (team_id, name, description, owner_id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (team_data["teamId"], name, description, user_id)
    )
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO team_members (team_id, user_id, role, joined_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (team_data["teamId"], user_id, "owner")
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "teamId": team_data["teamId"]}

@app.post("/teams/{team_id}/members")
async def add_team_member(team_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    new_member_id = data["newMemberId"]
    role = data.get("role", "member")

    # 1. Tambah anggota di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/{team_id}/members",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Authorization": f"Bearer {data['userToken']}"},
        json={"userId": new_member_id, "role": role}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to add team member")

    # 2. Simpan anggota ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO team_members (team_id, user_id, role, joined_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (team_id, new_member_id, role)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success"}

@app.get("/teams/{team_id}/assets")
async def list_team_assets(team_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_token = data["userToken"]

    # 1. Ambil asset dari 1Min.AI
    response = requests.get(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/teams/{team_id}/assets",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Authorization": f"Bearer {user_token}"}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Team or assets not found")

    assets = response.json()["assets"]

    # 2. Simpan asset ke database lokal (jika belum ada)
    for asset in assets:
        cursor.execute(
            "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO team_assets (asset_id, team_id, asset_url, type, uploaded_by, uploaded_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (
                asset["assetId"],
                team_id,
                asset["assetUrl"],
                asset["type"],
                asset["uploadedBy"],
                asset["uploadedAt"]
            )
        )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "assets": assets}
```

---
### **7.3 Modul Keamanan Tambahan**
Untuk memastikan integrasi **secure-by-default** dan **zero-trust compliant**, tambahkan:

#### **1. Validasi Permission**
- Pastikan pengguna memiliki *permission* yang cukup untuk melakukan tindakan (misal: menghapus *post*, mengelola tim).

**Contoh Middleware (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException

async def verify_team_permission(request: Request, team_id: str, required_role: str):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    user_token = data["userToken"]

    # 1. Ambil role pengguna di tim
    cursor.execute(
        "SELECT role FROM team_members WHERE team_id = ? AND user_id = ?",
        (team_id, user_id)
    )
    result = cursor.fetchone()
    if not result:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="User not in team")

    user_role = result[0]
    role_hierarchy = {"owner": 3, "admin": 2, "member": 1, "viewer": 0}
    if role_hierarchy.get(user_role, 0) < role_hierarchy.get(required_role, 0):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Insufficient permissions")

    return user_id
```

#### **2. Rate Limiting untuk AI Social**
- Batasi jumlah *post*, *like*, dan *comment* per pengguna.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/social/posts")
@limiter.limit("20/minute")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    ...
```

#### **3. Content Moderation**
- Moderasi konten *post* dan *comment* untuk menghindari *spam*, *hate speech*, atau konten berbahaya.

**Contoh (Python + Profanity Filter):**
```python
from profanity_check import predict, predict_prob

def is_content_safe(content: str) -> bool:
    # Cek profanity
    if predict([content])[0] == 1:
        return False

    # Cek panjang konten
    if len(content) > 1000:
        return False

    return True

@app.post("/social/posts")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    content = data.get("content", "")

    if not is_content_safe(content):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Content violates community guidelines")

    ...
```

#### **4. Audit Logging untuk AI Social**
- Catat semua aktivitas *post*, *like*, *comment*, dan *team management*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import logging

logging.basicConfig(filename='ai_social.log', level=logging.INFO)

def log_social_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    logging.info(
        f"{datetime.now()} | User: {user_id} | Action: {action} | Details: {details}"
    )

@app.post("/social/posts")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    post_id = data.get("postId")

    log_social_activity(user_id, "create_post", {"postId": post_id})
    ...
```

### **8.1 Alur Kerja: Berbagi Konten AI di AI Social**
1. **User Unggah Gambar:**

   ```python
   # Unggah gambar ke Asset API
   ```

   ```python
   response = requests.get(
   ```

1. **Owner Buat Tim:**

   ```python
   response = requests.post(
   ```

   - User mendapatkan **100 kredit gratis**.

## **🔒 SEKSI 9: KEAMANAN & COMPLIANCE UNTUK AI SOCIAL & COLLABORATION**
---
---

### **9.1 Zero Trust untuk AI Social**
- **Verifikasi Semua Akses:**
  - Pastikan hanya anggota tim yang berwenang yang dapat mengakses *asset* atau *post* tim.
- **Enkripsi Data Sensitif:**
  - Enkripsi *post* atau *comment* yang mengandung data sensitif.

**Contoh Middleware (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException
import jwt

SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"

async def verify_user(request: Request):
    token = request.headers.get("Authorization")
    if not token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing token")

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token.split(" ")[1], SECRET_KEY, algorithms=["HS256"])
        return payload["user_id"]
    except:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token")

@app.post("/social/posts")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    user_id = await verify_user(request)
    data = await request.json()
    if data["userId"] != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Unauthorized")
    ...
```

---
### **9.2 Replay Protection untuk AI Social**
- **Gunakan Nonce:**
  - Tambahkan `nonce` untuk setiap permintaan *post*, *like*, atau *comment*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import secrets

def generate_nonce() -> str:
    return secrets.token_hex(16)

@app.post("/social/posts")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    nonce = data.get("nonce")
    if not nonce or not validate_nonce(nonce):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid nonce")
    ...
```

---
### **9.3 Immutable Logging untuk AI Social**
- **Simpan Semua Aktivitas ke IPFS:**
  - Gunakan **IPFS** untuk *tamper-proof logging* aktivitas *post*, *like*, *comment*, dan *team management*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import ipfshttpclient

client = ipfshttpclient.connect()

def log_social_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    log_entry = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "user_id": user_id,
        "action": action,
        "details": details
    }
    log_hash = client.add_json(log_entry)
    return f"ipfs://{log_hash}"
```

---
### **9.4 Compliance (GDPR, PDP)**
- **Hapus Data Lama:**
  - Hapus *post*, *comment*, dan *asset* yang sudah tidak aktif (misal: > 30 hari).
- **Anonimisasi Data:**
  - Anonimisasi data pengguna dalam *post* atau *comment*.

**Contoh:**
```python
def anonymize_post(post: dict) -> dict:
    if "userId" in post:
        post["userId"] = "*****"
    if "userName" in post:
        post["userName"] = "*****"
    return post
```

---
### **9.5 Content Moderation untuk AI Social**
- **Filter Konten Berbahaya:**
  - Gunakan *profanity filter*, *hate speech detection*, dan *spam detection*.

**Contoh (Python + Hugging Face):**
```python
from transformers import pipeline

# Load model untuk deteksi konten berbahaya
toxic_classifier = pipeline("text-classification", model="facebook/roberta-hate-speech-dynabench-r4-target")

def is_content_safe(content: str) -> bool:
    # Cek toxic/hate speech
    result = toxic_classifier(content)
    if result[0]["label"] == "hate":
        return False

    # Cek panjang konten
    if len(content) > 1000:
        return False

    return True

@app.post("/social/posts")
async def create_post(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    content = data.get("content", "")

    if not is_content_safe(content):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Content violates community guidelines")

    ...
```
