### **🔹 Ringkasan Part 8**
- **AI Social:**
  - Platform kolaborasi untuk berbagi konten AI (gambar, video, teks).
  - Endpoint: `/posts`, `/posts/{postId}/like`, `/posts/{postId}/comments`, `/users/{userId}/follow`, `/feed`.
  - Fitur: *Post*, *Like*, *Comment*, *Follow*, *Feed*.
- **Multi-AI Chat:**
  - Berinteraksi dengan **beberapa model AI sekaligus**.
  - Parameter: `models`, `combine`.
  - Contoh: Gabungkan respons dari `GPT-4o` dan `Claude-4-6-Sonnet`.
- **Referral System:**
  - Dapatkan kredit gratis dengan mengundang teman.
  - Endpoint: `/referrals`, `/referrals/claim`.
  - Fitur: *Referral Link*, *Claim Reward*, *Leaderboard*.
- **Leaderboard:**
  - Paparkan *top contributors*, *top creators*, *top engagers*.
  - Endpoint: `/leaderboard`, `/leaderboard/referrals`, `/leaderboard/posts`.
- **Team Collaboration:**
  - Kelola tim, anggota, *permission*, dan *asset* bersama.
  - Endpoint: `/teams`, `/teams/{teamId}/members`, `/teams/{teamId}/assets`.
  - Role: `owner`, `admin`, `member`, `viewer`.
- **Integrasi dengan MusGo-OS 2in1:**
  - Arsitektur: API Gateway → 1Min.AI AI Social API → Database Lokal.
  - Contoh implementasi: FastAPI + SQLite + Background Tasks.
- **Contoh Alur Kerja:**
  - Berbagi konten AI di AI Social.
  - Kolaborasi tim dalam proyek AI.
  - Referral & Leaderboard.
- **Keamanan & Compliance:**
  - **Zero Trust** (verifikasi akses, enkripsi data).
  - **Replay Protection** (nonce).
  - **Immutable Logging** (IPFS/blockchain).
  - **Compliance** (GDPR, PDP: hapus data lama, anonimisasi).
  - **Content Moderation** (filter konten berbahaya).

## **🤖 SEKSI 1: IKHTISAR AI MODELS & CUSTOMIZATION**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **AI Models & Customization** untuk:
1. **Daftar Model AI** – Akses **75+ model AI** (Claude, GPT, Gemini, Llama, dll.) dengan kemampuan yang beragam.
2. **Model Customization** – *Fine-tuning* model AI untuk kebutuhan spesifik (misal: *domain-specific knowledge*, *custom tone*).
3. **Model Deployment** – *Deploy* model kustom ke *endpoint* pribadi atau publik.
4. **Model Monitoring** – Pantau performa, penggunaan, dan *health* model.
5. **Model Versioning** – Kelola versi model untuk *rollback* dan *A/B testing*.

**Kasus Penggunaan:**
- **Pemilihan Model Optimal:** Pilih model terbaik untuk tugas tertentu (misal: `GPT-4o` untuk *reasoning*, `Claude-4-6-Sonnet` untuk *coding*).
- **Fine-Tuning Model:** Sesuaikan model untuk *domain* atau *tone* spesifik (misal: model untuk *medical chatbot*).
- **Deploy Model Kustom:** *Deploy* model yang sudah di-*fine-tune* ke produksi.
- **Monitoring Model:** Pantau *latency*, *error rate*, dan *usage* model.

**1Min.AI** menyediakan **katalog model AI** yang dapat diakses via:
- **Endpoint `/api/models`** – Daftar semua model yang tersedia.
- **Filtering** – Filter model berdasarkan *provider*, *type*, *capability*, atau *price*.
- **Model Details** – Ambil detail spesifik untuk setiap model (misal: *context window*, *max tokens*, *pricing*).

### **2.7 Praktik Terbaik untuk Pemilihan Model**
1. **Pilih Model Berdasarkan Tugas:**
   - **Chat/Reasoning:** `GPT-4o`, `Claude-4-6-Sonnet`, `Gemini-3.1-Pro`.
   - **Coding:** `Claude-4-6-Sonnet`, `Qwen3-Coder-Plus`, `DeepSeek-Coder`.
   - **Image Generation:** `DALL·E 3`, `Flux`, `Stable Diffusion`.
   - **Audio:** `Whisper-1`, `Gemini-3.1-TTS`.
2. **Perhatikan Batasan:**
   - **Context Window:** Pilih model dengan *context window* yang cukup (misal: `GPT-4o` = 128K tokens).
   - **Rate Limits:** Perhatikan *rate limits* model (misal: `GPT-4o` = 1000 RPM).
3. **Optimalkan Biaya:**
   - Gunakan model yang lebih murah untuk tugas sederhana (misal: `GPT-4o-Mini` untuk chat umum).
4. **Uji Model:**
   - Lakukan *benchmarking* model untuk tugas spesifik sebelum *deploy* ke produksi.

## **⚙️ SEKSI 3: MODEL CUSTOMIZATION (FINE-TUNING & PENAMBAHAN PENGETAHUAN)**
---

### **3.1 Konsep Model Customization**
**1Min.AI** memungkinkan **customization model AI** untuk:
1. **Fine-Tuning:** Latih model dengan *dataset* khusus untuk meningkatkan performa pada *domain* spesifik.
2. **Prompt Engineering:** Sesuaikan *prompt template* untuk respons yang lebih konsisten.
3. **Knowledge Base:** Tambahkan *knowledge base* (misal: dokumen, FAQ) untuk model.
4. **Custom Instructions:** Berikan instruksi kustom untuk model (misal: *"Selalu jawab dalam Bahasa Indonesia"*).

---
### **3.2 Endpoint Model Customization**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/custom-models`             | `POST` | Buat model kustom baru.                    | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}`   | `GET`  | Ambil detail model kustom.                | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}`   | `PUT`  | Perbarui model kustom.                    | `curl -X PUT "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}`   | `DELETE`| Hapus model kustom.                      | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/fine-tune` | `POST` | Mulai *fine-tuning* model.          | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/fine-tune"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/knowledge` | `POST` | Tambah *knowledge base* ke model.   | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/knowledge"` |

---
### **3.3 Buat Model Kustom (Create Custom Model)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `name`                  | string  | Nama model kustom.                                                                           | ✅     |
| `baseModel`             | string  | Model dasar yang akan di-*fine-tune* (misal: `gpt-4o-mini`).                                | ✅     |
| `description`           | string  | Deskripsi model kustom.                                                                     | ❌     |
| `customInstructions`   | string  | Instruksi kustom untuk model.                                                               | ❌     |
| `promptTemplate`        | string  | *Prompt template* untuk model.                                                              | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "name": "MusGo-OS Assistant",
  "baseModel": "gpt-4o-mini",
  "description": "Model kustom untuk asisten MusGo-OS 2in1 dengan pengetahuan tentang cybersecurity dan AI.",
  "customInstructions": "Kamu adalah asisten untuk MusGo-OS 2in1. Selalu jawab dalam Bahasa Indonesia formal. Berikan jawaban yang detail dan teknis.",
  "promptTemplate": "Kamu adalah {role}. User: {user}. Pertanyaan: {prompt}. Berikan jawaban yang {style}."
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "name": "MusGo-OS Assistant",
  "baseModel": "gpt-4o-mini",
  "description": "Model kustom untuk asisten MusGo-OS 2in1...",
  "customInstructions": "Kamu adalah asisten untuk MusGo-OS 2in1...",
  "promptTemplate": "Kamu adalah {role}...",
  "status": "draft",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---
### **3.4 Fine-Tuning Model**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{modelId}/fine-tune
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `trainingData`          | string  | URL *dataset* untuk *fine-tuning* (format: JSONL, CSV, atau Parquet).                       | ✅     |
| `validationData`        | string  | URL *dataset* untuk validasi (opsional).                                                    | ❌     |
| `epochs`                | number  | Jumlah *epoch* untuk *fine-tuning*.                                                          | ❌     |
| `learningRate`          | number  | *Learning rate* untuk *fine-tuning*.                                                        | ❌     |
| `batchSize`             | number  | *Batch size* untuk *fine-tuning*.                                                            | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/fine-tune' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "trainingData": "https://storage.1min.ai/datasets/musgo_os_training.jsonl",
  "validationData": "https://storage.1min.ai/datasets/musgo_os_validation.jsonl",
  "epochs": 3,
  "learningRate": 0.0001,
  "batchSize": 32
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "fineTuneId": "ft_xyz789",
  "status": "queued",
  "estimatedCompletionTime": 3600,
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:05:00Z"
}
```

**Status Fine-Tuning:**
| Status          | Deskripsi                                                                                     |
|-----------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `queued`        | *Fine-tuning* sedang menunggu di *queue*.                                                    |
| `running`       | *Fine-tuning* sedang berjalan.                                                               |
| `completed`     | *Fine-tuning* selesai. Model siap digunakan.                                                 |
| `failed`        | *Fine-tuning* gagal.                                                                         |
| `cancelled`     | *Fine-tuning* dibatalkan.                                                                     |

---
### **3.5 Tambah Knowledge Base ke Model**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{modelId}/knowledge
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `knowledgeBase`         | array   | Daftar *knowledge base* (dokumen, FAQ, dll.) dalam format teks.                             | ✅     |
| `embeddingModel`        | string  | Model untuk *embedding* (misal: `text-embedding-3-small`).                                  | ❌     |
| `chunkSize`             | number  | Ukuran *chunk* untuk *knowledge base* (default: 512 tokens).                                | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/knowledge' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "knowledgeBase": [
    {
      "title": "Dokumentasi MusGo-OS 2in1",
      "content": "MusGo-OS 2in1 adalah sistem operasi AI yang dirancang untuk keamanan, skalabilitas, dan kolaborasi...",
      "source": "https://docs.musgo-os.example.com"
    },
    {
      "title": "FAQ Cybersecurity",
      "content": "Q: Apa itu Zero Trust Architecture? A: Zero Trust Architecture adalah model keamanan yang berasumsi bahwa setiap akses harus divalidasi...",
      "source": "https://faq.musgo-os.example.com"
    }
  ],
  "embeddingModel": "text-embedding-3-small",
  "chunkSize": 512
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "knowledgeBaseId": "kb_def456",
  "status": "processing",
  "estimatedCompletionTime": 600,
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:10:00Z"
}
```

---
### **3.6 Praktik Terbaik untuk Model Customization**
1. **Pilih Base Model yang Tepat:**
   - Gunakan model yang sudah mendekati kebutuhan (misal: `GPT-4o-Mini` untuk *fine-tuning* chatbot).
2. **Siapkan Dataset Berkualitas:**
   - Pastikan *dataset* bersih, terstruktur, dan relevan dengan *domain*.
3. **Uji Model Setelah Fine-Tuning:**
   - Lakukan *evaluation* untuk memastikan performa model meningkat.
4. **Monitoring Model:**
   - Pantau *latency*, *error rate*, dan *usage* model kustom.
5. **Versioning Model:**
   - Simpan versi model untuk *rollback* jika diperlukan.

## **🚀 SEKSI 4: MODEL DEPLOYMENT (DEPLOY MODEL KUSTOM)**
---

### **4.1 Konsep Model Deployment**
**1Min.AI** memungkinkan **deployment model kustom** ke:
1. **Endpoint Pribadi:** *Deploy* model ke *endpoint* yang hanya dapat diakses oleh Anda.
2. **Endpoint Publik:** *Deploy* model ke *endpoint* yang dapat diakses oleh publik (dengan autentikasi).
3. **On-Premise:** *Deploy* model ke infrastruktur lokal (misal: Kubernetes, Docker).

---
### **4.2 Endpoint Model Deployment**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/deploy` | `POST` | *Deploy* model kustom.                     | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/deploy"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/deployments` | `GET` | Daftar *deployment* model.               | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/deployments"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/deployments/{deploymentId}` | `GET` | Ambil detail *deployment*.               | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/deployments/d_abc123"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/deployments/{deploymentId}` | `DELETE` | Hapus *deployment*.                     | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/deployments/d_abc123"` |

---
### **4.3 Deploy Model Kustom**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{modelId}/deploy
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `deploymentName`        | string  | Nama *deployment*.                                                                           | ✅     |
| `visibility`            | string  | Visibilitas (`private`, `public`).                                                           | ✅     |
| `scaling`               | object  | Konfigurasi *scaling* (misal: `minReplicas`, `maxReplicas`).                                | ❌     |
| `authentication`        | object  | Konfigurasi autentikasi (`apiKey`, `jwt`).                                                    | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/deploy' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "deploymentName": "MusGo-OS Assistant Production",
  "visibility": "private",
  "scaling": {
    "minReplicas": 1,
    "maxReplicas": 5
  },
  "authentication": {
    "type": "apiKey",
    "apiKey": "your-deployment-api-key"
  }
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "deploymentId": "d_abc123",
  "deploymentName": "MusGo-OS Assistant Production",
  "endpoint": "https://cm_abc123.api.1min.ai",
  "visibility": "private",
  "status": "deploying",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:15:00Z",
  "estimatedCompletionTime": 300
}
```

**Status Deployment:**
| Status          | Deskripsi                                                                                     |
|-----------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `deploying`     | *Deployment* sedang berjalan.                                                                |
| `active`        | *Deployment* aktif dan siap digunakan.                                                       |
| `failed`        | *Deployment* gagal.                                                                           |
| `scaling`       | *Deployment* sedang *scaling* (menyesuaikan jumlah *replica*).                                |
| `paused`        | *Deployment* dijeda.                                                                         |

---
### **4.4 Gunakan Model yang Sudah Di-Deploy**
Setelah *deploy*, model kustom dapat digunakan via **endpoint yang disediakan**:
```
POST https://cm_abc123.api.1min.ai/chat
```
**Header:**
```http
Authorization: Bearer <deployment_api_key>
Content-Type: application/json
```
**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://cm_abc123.api.1min.ai/chat' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer your-deployment-api-key' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "prompt": "Jelaskan tentang Zero Trust Architecture dalam MusGo-OS 2in1.",
  "conversationId": "conv_123"
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "response": "Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA) dalam MusGo-OS 2in1 adalah implementasi model keamanan yang berasumsi bahwa setiap akses ke sistem harus divalidasi, terlepas dari lokasi atau jaringan asalnya. Dalam konteks MusGo-OS 2in1, ZTA diterapkan dengan...",
  "conversationId": "conv_123",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "usage": {
    "promptTokens": 25,
    "completionTokens": 200,
    "totalTokens": 225
  }
}
```

---
### **4.5 Praktik Terbaik untuk Model Deployment**
1. **Uji Deployment di Lingkungan Staging:**
   - *Deploy* model ke lingkungan *staging* terlebih dahulu untuk pengujian.
2. **Monitoring Deployment:**
   - Pantau *latency*, *error rate*, dan *usage* model yang di-*deploy*.
3. **Scaling Otomatis:**
   - Konfigurasikan *auto-scaling* untuk menangani lonjakan permintaan.
4. **Backup & Rollback:**
   - Simpan *backup* model dan siapkan *rollback plan* jika *deployment* gagal.
5. **Keamanan Deployment:**
   - Gunakan autentikasi yang kuat (misal: `JWT`, `API Key`).
   - Batasi akses ke *endpoint* (misal: *IP whitelisting*).

## **📊 SEKSI 5: MODEL MONITORING & VERSIONING**
---
---

### **5.1 Model Monitoring**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **monitoring** untuk model kustom, termasuk:
- **Usage Metrics:** Jumlah permintaan, *token usage*, *latency*.
- **Error Metrics:** *Error rate*, *timeout*, *rate limits*.
- **Health Metrics:** Status *deployment*, *replica health*.

**Endpoint Monitoring:**
```
GET https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{modelId}/monitoring
```
**Parameter Query Opsional:**
| Parameter   | Tipe    | Deskripsi                          | Contoh               |
|-------------|---------|------------------------------------|----------------------|
| `startDate` | string  | Tanggal mulai (ISO 8601).          | `startDate=2026-05-01` |
| `endDate`   | string  | Tanggal akhir (ISO 8601).          | `endDate=2026-05-19` |
| `metric`    | string  | Metrik yang ingin dipantau (`usage`, `error`, `health`). | `metric=usage` |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/monitoring?startDate=2026-05-01&endDate=2026-05-19&metric=usage' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "metric": "usage",
  "data": [
    {
      "date": "2026-05-01",
      "requests": 100,
      "inputTokens": 50000,
      "outputTokens": 200000,
      "latencyAvg": 1200,
      "latencyMax": 3000
    },
    {
      "date": "2026-05-02",
      "requests": 150,
      "inputTokens": 75000,
      "outputTokens": 300000,
      "latencyAvg": 1500,
      "latencyMax": 4000
    }
  ]
}
```

---
### **5.2 Model Versioning**
**1Min.AI** mendukung **versioning** untuk model kustom, memungkinkan:
- **Rollback** ke versi sebelumnya jika *deployment* gagal.
- **A/B Testing** dengan beberapa versi model.
- **Version Aliases** (misal: `latest`, `stable`).

**Endpoint Versioning:**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/versions` | `GET` | Daftar versi model.                      | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/versions"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/versions/{versionId}` | `GET` | Ambil detail versi model.               | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/versions/v_1"` |
| `/custom-models/{modelId}/versions/{versionId}/rollback` | `POST` | *Rollback* ke versi ini.               | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/versions/v_1/rollback"` |

**Contoh Permintaan (Daftar Versi):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/versions' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "versions": [
    {
      "versionId": "v_1",
      "version": "1.0.0",
      "description": "Versi awal model MusGo-OS Assistant.",
      "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z",
      "status": "active",
      "isLatest": false
    },
    {
      "versionId": "v_2",
      "version": "1.1.0",
      "description": "Versi dengan peningkatan pada pengetahuan cybersecurity.",
      "createdAt": "2026-05-19T13:00:00Z",
      "status": "active",
      "isLatest": true
    }
  ]
}
```

**Contoh Permintaan (Rollback):**
```bash
curl --location -X POST 'https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/cm_abc123/versions/v_1/rollback' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "modelId": "cm_abc123",
  "versionId": "v_1",
  "message": "Model rolled back to version 1.0.0",
  "currentVersion": "1.0.0"
}
```

---
### **5.3 Praktik Terbaik untuk Model Versioning**
1. **Berikan Deskripsi Versi yang Jelas:**
   - Catat perubahan yang dilakukan pada setiap versi.
2. **Uji Versi Baru Sebelum Deploy:**
   - Lakukan *testing* menyeluruh sebelum *deploy* versi baru.
3. **Gunakan Version Aliases:**
   - Gunakan `latest` untuk versi terbaru dan `stable` untuk versi stabil.
4. **Rollback Plan:**
   - Siapkan *rollback plan* jika versi baru menyebabkan masalah.
5. **Hapus Versi Lama:**
   - Hapus versi lama yang sudah tidak digunakan untuk menghemat ruang.

---
---
## **🔌 SEKSI 6: INTEGRASI AI MODELS & CUSTOMIZATION DENGAN MUSGO-OS 2IN1**
---
---

### **6.1 Arsitektur Integrasi**
**MusGo-OS 2in1** dapat mengintegrasikan **AI Models & Customization** untuk:
1. **Modul Pemilihan Model:**
   - Pilih model terbaik untuk tugas tertentu (misal: `GPT-4o` untuk *reasoning*, `Claude-4-6-Sonnet` untuk *coding*).
2. **Modul Fine-Tuning:**
   - *Fine-tune* model untuk *domain* spesifik (misal: cybersecurity, AI governance).
3. **Modul Deployment:**
   - *Deploy* model kustom ke *endpoint* internal **MusGo-OS 2in1**.
4. **Modul Monitoring:**
   - Pantau performa dan *health* model yang digunakan.

**Contoh Arsitektur:**
```
[User] → [MusGo-OS 2in1] → (Model Selector) → [1Min.AI Model Catalog] → (Pilih Model)
[MusGo-OS 2in1] → (Fine-Tuning Service) → [1Min.AI Custom Models] → (Fine-Tune Model)
[MusGo-OS 2in1] → (Deployment Service) → [1Min.AI Model Deployment] → (Deploy Model)
[MusGo-OS 2in1] → (Monitoring Service) → [1Min.AI Model Monitoring] → (Pantau Model)
```

---
### **6.2 Contoh Implementasi (Python + FastAPI)**
**1. Setup Endpoint untuk Pemilihan Model:**
```python
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, HTTPException
import requests
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI()
DB_FILE = "musgo_models.db"

# Setup database
conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS model_usage (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        model_id TEXT,
        task_type TEXT,
        user_id TEXT,
        usage_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        last_used TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.get("/models")
async def list_models(request: Request):
    # Ambil daftar model dari 1Min.AI
    response = requests.get(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/models",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to fetch models")

    models = response.json()["models"]

    # Tambahkan informasi penggunaan dari database lokal
    for model in models:
        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT usage_count, last_used FROM model_usage WHERE model_id = ?",
            (model["modelId"],)
        )
        result = cursor.fetchone()
        if result:
            model["usageCount"] = result[0]
            model["lastUsed"] = result[1]

    return {"status": "success", "models": models}

@app.post("/models/select")
async def select_model(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    model_id = data["modelId"]
    task_type = data["taskType"]
    user_id = data["userId"]

    # Catat penggunaan model
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO model_usage (model_id, task_type, user_id, usage_count, last_used) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(model_id, task_type, user_id) DO UPDATE SET usage_count = usage_count + 1, last_used = datetime('now')",
        (model_id, task_type, user_id)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "modelId": model_id}
```

**2. Setup Endpoint untuk Fine-Tuning Model:**
```python
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fine_tuning_jobs (
        job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        model_id TEXT,
        training_data_url TEXT,
        status TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        completed_at TIMESTAMP,
        result_model_id TEXT
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/models/fine-tune")
async def fine_tune_model(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    model_id = data["modelId"]
    training_data_url = data["trainingDataUrl"]
    user_id = data["userId"]

    # 1. Buat model kustom di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "name": f"Fine-Tuned {model_id} by {user_id}",
            "baseModel": model_id,
            "description": f"Model fine-tuned untuk tugas {data.get('taskType', 'general')}."
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to create custom model")

    custom_model_id = response.json()["modelId"]

    # 2. Mulai fine-tuning
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/fine-tune",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "trainingData": training_data_url,
            "epochs": data.get("epochs", 3),
            "learningRate": data.get("learningRate", 0.0001)
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to start fine-tuning")

    fine_tune_data = response.json()

    # 3. Simpan job fine-tuning ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO fine_tuning_jobs (job_id, model_id, training_data_url, status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (
            fine_tune_data["fineTuneId"],
            model_id,
            training_data_url,
            fine_tune_data["status"]
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "jobId": fine_tune_data["fineTuneId"], "customModelId": custom_model_id}

@app.get("/models/fine-tune/{job_id}")
async def get_fine_tune_status(job_id: str):
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM fine_tuning_jobs WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,))
    job = cursor.fetchone()
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")

    # Ambil status dari 1Min.AI
    response = requests.get(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{job[1]}/fine-tune/{job[0]}",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to fetch fine-tune status")

    status_data = response.json()

    # Perbarui status di database
    cursor.execute(
        "UPDATE fine_tuning_jobs SET status = ?, completed_at = ? WHERE job_id = ?",
        (status_data["status"], status_data.get("completedAt"), job_id)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "statusData": status_data}
```

**3. Setup Endpoint untuk Deployment Model:**
```python
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS model_deployments (
        deployment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        model_id TEXT,
        deployment_name TEXT,
        endpoint TEXT,
        status TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/models/deploy")
async def deploy_model(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    model_id = data["modelId"]
    deployment_name = data["deploymentName"]
    visibility = data.get("visibility", "private")

    # 1. Deploy model di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{model_id}/deploy",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "deploymentName": deployment_name,
            "visibility": visibility,
            "scaling": data.get("scaling", {"minReplicas": 1, "maxReplicas": 3})
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to deploy model")

    deployment_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan deployment ke database
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO model_deployments (deployment_id, model_id, deployment_name, endpoint, status, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), datetime('now'))",
        (
            deployment_data["deploymentId"],
            model_id,
            deployment_name,
            deployment_data["endpoint"],
            deployment_data["status"]
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "deploymentId": deployment_data["deploymentId"], "endpoint": deployment_data["endpoint"]}

@app.get("/models/deployments")
async def list_deployments():
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM model_deployments")
    deployments = []
    for row in cursor.fetchall():
        deployments.append({
            "deploymentId": row[0],
            "modelId": row[1],
            "deploymentName": row[2],
            "endpoint": row[3],
            "status": row[4],
            "createdAt": row[5],
            "updatedAt": row[6]
        })
    return {"status": "success", "deployments": deployments}
```

---
### **6.3 Modul Keamanan Tambahan**
Untuk memastikan integrasi **secure-by-default** dan **zero-trust compliant**, tambahkan:

#### **1. Validasi Model ID**
- Pastikan `modelId` yang digunakan adalah **model yang valid** dari **1Min.AI**.

**Contoh:**
```python
def is_valid_model(model_id: str) -> bool:
    response = requests.get(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/models/{model_id}",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
    )
    return response.status_code == 200
```

#### **2. Rate Limiting untuk Fine-Tuning & Deployment**
- Batasi jumlah *fine-tuning* dan *deployment* per pengguna.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/models/fine-tune")
@limiter.limit("5/hour")
async def fine_tune_model(request: Request):
    ...
```

#### **3. Enkripsi Data Training**
- Enkripsi *dataset* yang digunakan untuk *fine-tuning*.

**Contoh (Python + Fernet):**
```python
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

key = Fernet.generate_key()
cipher_suite = Fernet(key)

def encrypt_training_data(data: str) -> str:
    return cipher_suite.encrypt(data.encode()).decode()

def decrypt_training_data(encrypted_data: str) -> str:
    return cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_data.encode()).decode()
```

#### **4. Audit Logging untuk Model Customization**
- Catat semua aktivitas *fine-tuning*, *deployment*, dan *versioning*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import logging

logging.basicConfig(filename='model_customization.log', level=logging.INFO)

def log_model_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    logging.info(
        f"{datetime.now()} | User: {user_id} | Action: {action} | Details: {details}"
    )

@app.post("/models/fine-tune")
async def fine_tune_model(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data["userId"]
    log_model_activity(user_id, "fine_tune", {"modelId": data["modelId"], "trainingDataUrl": data["trainingDataUrl"]})
    ...
```

## **📊 SEKSI 7: CONTOH ALUR KERJA (WORKFLOW)**
---
---

### **7.1 Alur Kerja: Fine-Tuning Model untuk Domain Cybersecurity**
1. **Pilih Base Model:**
   ```python
   # Ambil daftar model
   response = requests.get(
       "https://api.1min.ai/api/models?capability=chat&type=text",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
   )
   models = response.json()["models"]
   base_model = "gpt-4o-mini"  # Pilih model dengan biaya rendah
   ```

2. **Buat Model Kustom:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       "https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "name": "Cybersecurity Assistant",
           "baseModel": base_model,
           "description": "Model kustom untuk asisten cybersecurity dengan pengetahuan tentang Zero Trust, SOC, dan AI Security.",
           "customInstructions": "Kamu adalah asisten cybersecurity. Selalu jawab dalam Bahasa Indonesia formal. Berikan jawaban yang detail dan teknis."
       }
   )
   custom_model_id = response.json()["modelId"]
   ```

3. **Unggah Dataset Training:**
   ```python
   # Unggah dataset ke 1Min.AI Asset API
   response = requests.post(
       "https://api.1min.ai/api/assets/upload",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"},
       files={"file": open("cybersecurity_dataset.jsonl", "rb")},
       data={"type": "document"}
   )
   training_data_url = response.json()["assetUrl"]
   ```

4. **Mulai Fine-Tuning:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/fine-tune",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "trainingData": training_data_url,
           "epochs": 3,
           "learningRate": 0.0001
       }
   )
   fine_tune_id = response.json()["fineTuneId"]
   ```

5. **Pantau Status Fine-Tuning:**
   ```python
   while True:
       response = requests.get(
           f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/fine-tune/{fine_tune_id}",
           headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
       )
       status = response.json()["status"]
       if status in ["completed", "failed"]:
           break
       time.sleep(60)  # Cek setiap 1 menit
   ```

6. **Deploy Model yang Sudah Di-Fine-Tune:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/deploy",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "deploymentName": "Cybersecurity Assistant Production",
           "visibility": "private",
           "scaling": {"minReplicas": 1, "maxReplicas": 3}
       }
   )
   endpoint = response.json()["endpoint"]
   ```

7. **Gunakan Model yang Sudah Di-Deploy:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       endpoint,
       headers={"Authorization": "Bearer DEPLOYMENT_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "prompt": "Jelaskan tentang Zero Trust Architecture dalam konteks MusGo-OS 2in1.",
           "conversationId": "conv_cyber_123"
       }
   )
   print(response.json()["response"])
   ```

---
### **7.2 Alur Kerja: A/B Testing dengan Beberapa Versi Model**
1. **Deploy Versi 1 Model:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/deploy",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "deploymentName": "Model v1",
           "visibility": "private"
       }
   )
   endpoint_v1 = response.json()["endpoint"]
   ```

2. **Fine-Tune Model untuk Versi 2:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/fine-tune",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "trainingData": "https://storage.1min.ai/datasets/improved_cybersecurity_dataset.jsonl",
           "epochs": 5
       }
   )
   ```

3. **Deploy Versi 2 Model:**
   ```python
   response = requests.post(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/deploy",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={
           "deploymentName": "Model v2",
           "visibility": "private"
       }
   )
   endpoint_v2 = response.json()["endpoint"]
   ```

4. **A/B Testing:**
   ```python
   # Kirim 50% permintaan ke v1 dan 50% ke v2
   import random

   def get_model_endpoint():
       return endpoint_v1 if random.random() < 0.5 else endpoint_v2

   # Contoh penggunaan
   endpoint = get_model_endpoint()
   response = requests.post(
       endpoint,
       headers={"Authorization": "Bearer DEPLOYMENT_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
       json={"prompt": "Jelaskan tentang SOC."}
   )
   print(response.json()["response"])
   ```

5. **Analisis Hasil A/B Testing:**
   ```python
   # Catat respons dan performa dari setiap versi
   results = {
       "v1": {"responses": [], "latency": []},
       "v2": {"responses": [], "latency": []}
   }

   for _ in range(100):
       start_time = time.time()
       endpoint = get_model_endpoint()
       version = "v1" if endpoint == endpoint_v1 else "v2"
       response = requests.post(
           endpoint,
           headers={"Authorization": "Bearer DEPLOYMENT_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
           json={"prompt": "Jelaskan tentang SOC."}
       )
       latency = time.time() - start_time
       results[version]["responses"].append(response.json()["response"])
       results[version]["latency"].append(latency)

   # Analisis hasil
   avg_latency_v1 = sum(results["v1"]["latency"]) / len(results["v1"]["latency"])
   avg_latency_v2 = sum(results["v2"]["latency"]) / len(results["v2"]["latency"])
   print(f"Average Latency v1: {avg_latency_v1:.2f}s")
   print(f"Average Latency v2: {avg_latency_v2:.2f}s")
   ```

6. **Pilih Versi Terbaik:**
   - Pilih versi dengan *latency* terendah dan *response quality* terbaik.

---
### **7.3 Alur Kerja: Monitoring & Rollback Model**
1. **Monitoring Model:**
   ```python
   response = requests.get(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/monitoring?startDate=2026-05-01&endDate=2026-05-19&metric=usage",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
   )
   monitoring_data = response.json()["data"]
   print(f"Usage Data: {monitoring_data}")
   ```

2. **Deteksi Anomali:**
   ```python
   # Contoh: Deteksi jika error rate > 5%
   error_rate_threshold = 0.05
   for day_data in monitoring_data:
       error_rate = day_data.get("errorRate", 0)
       if error_rate > error_rate_threshold:
           print(f"Anomali terdeteksi pada {day_data['date']}: Error rate = {error_rate:.2%}")
   ```

3. **Rollback ke Versi Sebelumnya:**
   ```python
   # Ambil daftar versi
   response = requests.get(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/versions",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
   )
   versions = response.json()["versions"]

   # Pilih versi stabil (misal: v1)
   stable_version = next(v for v in versions if v["version"] == "1.0.0")

   # Rollback ke versi stabil
   response = requests.post(
       f"https://api.1min.ai/api/custom-models/{custom_model_id}/versions/{stable_version['versionId']}/rollback",
       headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
   )
   print(f"Rollback ke versi {stable_version['version']} berhasil.")
   ```

## **🔒 SEKSI 8: KEAMANAN & COMPLIANCE UNTUK AI MODELS & CUSTOMIZATION**
---
---

### **8.1 Zero Trust untuk Model Customization**
- **Verifikasi Semua Akses:**
  - Pastikan hanya pengguna yang berwenang yang dapat melakukan *fine-tuning* atau *deployment*.
- **Enkripsi Data Training:**
  - Enkripsi *dataset* yang digunakan untuk *fine-tuning*.

**Contoh Middleware (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException
import jwt

SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"

async def verify_admin(request: Request):
    token = request.headers.get("Authorization")
    if not token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing token")

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token.split(" ")[1], SECRET_KEY, algorithms=["HS256"])
        if payload.get("role") != "admin":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Admin access required")
        return payload["user_id"]
    except:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token")

@app.post("/models/fine-tune")
async def fine_tune_model(request: Request):
    user_id = await verify_admin(request)
    ...
```

---
### **8.2 Replay Protection untuk Model Deployment**
- **Gunakan Nonce:**
  - Tambahkan `nonce` untuk setiap permintaan *deployment*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import secrets

def generate_nonce() -> str:
    return secrets.token_hex(16)

@app.post("/models/deploy")
async def deploy_model(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    nonce = data.get("nonce")
    if not nonce or not validate_nonce(nonce):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid nonce")
    ...
```

---
### **8.3 Immutable Logging untuk Model Customization**
- **Simpan Semua Aktivitas ke IPFS:**
  - Gunakan **IPFS** untuk *tamper-proof logging* aktivitas *fine-tuning*, *deployment*, dan *versioning*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import ipfshttpclient

client = ipfshttpclient.connect()

def log_model_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    log_entry = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "user_id": user_id,
        "action": action,
        "details": details
    }
    log_hash = client.add_json(log_entry)
    return f"ipfs://{log_hash}"
```

---
### **8.4 Compliance (GDPR, PDP)**
- **Hapus Data Lama:**
  - Hapus *dataset* dan *model* yang sudah tidak digunakan.
- **Anonimisasi Data Training:**
  - Anonimisasi data sensitif dalam *dataset* sebelum *fine-tuning*.

**Contoh:**
```python
def anonymize_dataset(dataset: list) -> list:
    anonymized_dataset = []
    for item in dataset:
        if "user_id" in item:
            item["user_id"] = "*****"
        if "email" in item:
            item["email"] = "*****@*****.com"
        anonymized_dataset.append(item)
    return anonymized_dataset
```

---
### **8.5 Model Security Hardening**
- **Validasi Input Model:**
  - Pastikan *input* yang dikirim ke model sudah divalidasi dan disanitasi.
- **Rate Limiting untuk Model:**
  - Batasi jumlah permintaan ke model untuk menghindari *abuse*.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/models/{model_id}/chat")
@limiter.limit("100/minute")
async def chat_with_model(model_id: str, request: Request):
    ...
```

### **🔹 Ringkasan Part 9**
- **AI Model Catalog:**
  - Daftar **75+ model AI** (Claude, GPT, Gemini, Llama, dll.).
  - Endpoint: `/models`, `/models/{modelId}`, `/models/search`.
  - Parameter: `provider`, `type`, `capability`, `limit`, `page`.
- **Model Customization:**
  - *Fine-tuning* model untuk *domain* spesifik.
  - Menambahkan *knowledge base* dan *custom instructions*.
  - Endpoint: `/custom-models`, `/custom-models/{modelId}/fine-tune`, `/custom-models/{modelId}/knowledge`.
  - Parameter: `baseModel`, `trainingData`, `epochs`, `knowledgeBase`.
- **Model Deployment:**
  - *Deploy* model kustom ke *endpoint* pribadi atau publik.
  - Endpoint: `/custom-models/{modelId}/deploy`, `/custom-models/{modelId}/deployments`.
  - Parameter: `deploymentName`, `visibility`, `scaling`, `authentication`.
- **Model Monitoring & Versioning:**
  - Pantau performa, *health*, dan *usage* model.
  - Kelola versi model untuk *rollback* dan *A/B testing*.
  - Endpoint: `/custom-models/{modelId}/monitoring`, `/custom-models/{modelId}/versions`.
- **Integrasi dengan MusGo-OS 2in1:**
  - Arsitektur: Model Selector → Customization Service → Deployment Service → Monitoring Service.
  - Contoh implementasi: FastAPI + SQLite + Background Tasks.
- **Contoh Alur Kerja:**
  - Fine-Tuning model untuk domain cybersecurity.
  - A/B Testing dengan beberapa versi model.
  - Monitoring & Rollback model.
- **Keamanan & Compliance:**
  - **Zero Trust** (verifikasi akses, enkripsi data).
  - **Replay Protection** (nonce).
  - **Immutable Logging** (IPFS/blockchain).
  - **Compliance** (GDPR, PDP: hapus data lama, anonimisasi).
  - **Model Security Hardening** (validasi input, rate limiting).

## **⚡ SEKSI 1: IKHTISAR AI WORKFLOWS & AUTOMATION**
**1Min.AI** menyediakan **AI Workflows & Automation** untuk:
1. **AI Workflows** – Rantai **beberapa fitur AI** menjadi alur kerja otomatis (misal: *transkripsi audio → terjemahan → ringkasan*).
2. **Automation Rules** – Atur *trigger* dan *action* untuk otomatisasi tugas (misal: *"Jika file diunggah, jalankan OCR dan simpan teks"*).
3. **Scheduled Workflows** – Jalankan *workflow* pada jadwal tertentu (misal: *ringkasan harian percakapan*).
4. **Webhook Triggers** – Pemicu *workflow* berdasarkan *event* eksternal (misal: *webhook* dari GitHub).
5. **Conditional Logic** – Tambahkan *if-else* dan *loop* dalam *workflow*.

**Kasus Penggunaan:**
- **Pemrosesan Dokumen Otomatis:** Unggah PDF → Ekstrak teks → Terjemahkan → Simpan ke database.
- **Generasi Konten Massal:** Buat 100 gambar dari *prompt* yang berbeda → Unggah ke CDN.
- **Monitoring & Alerting:** Pantau *error logs* → Kirim notifikasi ke Slack jika ditemukan *anomali*.
- **Data Pipeline:** Ambil data dari API → Bersihkan → Analisis → Visualisasi.

## **🔗 SEKSI 2: AI WORKFLOWS (RANTAI FITUR AI)**
---

### **2.1 Konsep AI Workflows**
**AI Workflows** memungkinkan Anda untuk:
- **Menggabungkan beberapa fitur AI** dalam satu alur kerja.
- **Menjalankan tugas secara berurutan** (sequential) atau **paralel** (parallel).
- **Menyimpan hasil intermediate** untuk digunakan di tahap selanjutnya.
- **Menangani kesalahan** (*error handling*) dan *retry* otomatis.

**Contoh Workflow:**
```
1. Unggah Audio → 2. Transkripsi (SPEECH_TO_TEXT) → 3. Terjemahan (TRANSLATION) → 4. Ringkasan (SUMMARIZATION) → 5. Simpan ke Database
```

---
### **2.2 Endpoint & Autentikasi**
**Base URL:**
```
https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows
```
**Header Wajib:**
| Field        | Value              | Keterangan                          |
|--------------|--------------------|------------------------------------|
| `API-KEY`    | `<your_api_key>`   | Kunci API dari dashboard 1Min.AI   |

---
### **2.3 Endpoint & Metode AI Workflows**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/`                          | `POST` | Buat *workflow* baru.                      | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows"` |
| `/{workflowId}`              | `GET`  | Ambil detail *workflow*.                  | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123"` |
| `/{workflowId}`              | `PUT`  | Perbarui *workflow*.                      | `curl -X PUT "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123"` |
| `/{workflowId}`              | `DELETE`| Hapus *workflow*.                        | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123"` |
| `/{workflowId}/run`          | `POST` | Jalankan *workflow*.                      | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/run"` |
| `/{workflowId}/runs`         | `GET`  | Daftar *run* dari *workflow*.             | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/runs"` |
| `/{workflowId}/runs/{runId}` | `GET`  | Ambil detail *run* *workflow*.            | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/runs/run_xyz789"` |

---
### **2.4 Buat Workflow (Create Workflow)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `name`                  | string  | Nama *workflow*.                                                                           | ✅     |
| `description`           | string  | Deskripsi *workflow*.                                                                       | ❌     |
| `steps`                 | array   | Daftar *step* dalam *workflow*.                                                             | ✅     |
| `trigger`               | object  | *Trigger* untuk *workflow* (misal: `manual`, `schedule`, `webhook`).                           | ❌     |

**Contoh `steps`:**
```json
"steps": [
  {
    "id": "step_1",
    "name": "Transkripsi Audio",
    "type": "SPEECH_TO_TEXT",
    "model": "whisper-1",
    "input": {
      "audioUrl": "{{input.audioUrl}}"
    },
    "output": "transcript"
  },
  {
    "id": "step_2",
    "name": "Terjemahan",
    "type": "TRANSLATION",
    "model": "gpt-4o",
    "input": {
      "prompt": "{{steps.step_1.output}}",
      "targetLanguage": "id"
    },
    "output": "translation"
  },
  {
    "id": "step_3",
    "name": "Ringkasan",
    "type": "SUMMARIZATION",
    "model": "gpt-4o",
    "input": {
      "prompt": "{{steps.step_2.output}}",
      "maxTokens": 500
    },
    "output": "summary"
  }
]
```

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "name": "Pemrosesan Audio Otomatis",
  "description": "Workflow untuk transkripsi, terjemahan, dan ringkasan audio.",
  "steps": [
    {
      "id": "step_1",
      "name": "Transkripsi Audio",
      "type": "SPEECH_TO_TEXT",
      "model": "whisper-1",
      "input": {
        "audioUrl": "{{input.audioUrl}}"
      },
      "output": "transcript"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_2",
      "name": "Terjemahan",
      "type": "TRANSLATION",
      "model": "gpt-4o",
      "input": {
        "prompt": "{{steps.step_1.output}}",
        "targetLanguage": "id"
      },
      "output": "translation"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_3",
      "name": "Ringkasan",
      "type": "SUMMARIZATION",
      "model": "gpt-4o",
      "input": {
        "prompt": "{{steps.step_2.output}}",
        "maxTokens": 500
      },
      "output": "summary"
    }
  ],
  "trigger": {
    "type": "manual"
  }
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "workflowId": "wf_abc123",
  "name": "Pemrosesan Audio Otomatis",
  "description": "Workflow untuk transkripsi, terjemahan, dan ringkasan audio.",
  "steps": [...],
  "trigger": {
    "type": "manual"
  },
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z",
  "updatedAt": "2026-05-19T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---
### **2.5 Jalankan Workflow (Run Workflow)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflowId}/run
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter | Tipe   | Deskripsi                          | Wajib? |
|-----------|--------|------------------------------------|--------|
| `input`   | object | *Input* untuk *workflow* (misal: `audioUrl`, `text`). | ✅     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/run' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "input": {
    "audioUrl": "https://assets.1min.ai/audio/meeting.mp3"
  }
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "runId": "run_xyz789",
  "workflowId": "wf_abc123",
  "status": "queued",
  "input": {
    "audioUrl": "https://assets.1min.ai/audio/meeting.mp3"
  },
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:05:00Z",
  "estimatedCompletionTime": 120
}
```

**Status Run:**
| Status          | Deskripsi                                                                                     |
|-----------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `queued`        | *Run* sedang menunggu di *queue*.                                                           |
| `running`       | *Run* sedang berjalan.                                                                        |
| `completed`     | *Run* selesai.                                                                               |
| `failed`        | *Run* gagal.                                                                                  |
| `cancelled`     | *Run* dibatalkan.                                                                            |

---
### **2.6 Ambil Hasil Workflow (Get Run Results)**
**Endpoint:**
```
GET https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflowId}/runs/{runId}
```
**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/runs/run_xyz789' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "runId": "run_xyz789",
  "workflowId": "wf_abc123",
  "status": "completed",
  "input": {
    "audioUrl": "https://assets.1min.ai/audio/meeting.mp3"
  },
  "output": {
    "step_1": {
      "status": "completed",
      "output": "Ini adalah transkripsi dari audio meeting...",
      "creditsUsed": 10
    },
    "step_2": {
      "status": "completed",
      "output": "Ini adalah terjemahan dari transkripsi...",
      "creditsUsed": 15
    },
    "step_3": {
      "status": "completed",
      "output": "Ringkasan: Meeting membahas tentang proyek AI...",
      "creditsUsed": 10
    }
  },
  "totalCreditsUsed": 35,
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:05:00Z",
  "completedAt": "2026-05-19T12:07:00Z"
}
```

---
### **2.7 Praktik Terbaik untuk AI Workflows**
1. **Rancang Workflow dengan Baik:**
   - Pisahkan *workflow* kompleks menjadi *step* yang lebih kecil.
   - Gunakan *output* dari satu *step* sebagai *input* untuk *step* berikutnya.
2. **Tangani Kesalahan:**
   - Tambahkan *error handling* untuk setiap *step* (misal: *retry* jika gagal).
3. **Optimalkan Biaya:**
   - Gunakan model yang lebih murah untuk *step* yang tidak memerlukan *high-end model*.
4. **Monitoring Workflow:**
   - Pantau *status* dan *performance* *workflow* secara berkala.
5. **Simpan Hasil Intermediate:**
   - Simpan *output* dari setiap *step* untuk debugging dan analisis.
