## **🤖 SEKSI 3: AUTOMATION RULES (OTOMATISASI TUGAS)**
---

### **3.1 Konsep Automation Rules**
**Automation Rules** memungkinkan Anda untuk:
- **Mendefinisikan *trigger*** (pemicu) dan *action* (tindakan) untuk otomatisasi.
- **Menjalankan *action* secara otomatis** saat *trigger* terpenuhi.
- **Menggunakan *webhook*** sebagai *trigger* eksternal.

**Contoh Automation Rule:**
- **Trigger:** *"File diunggah ke Asset API"*
- **Action:** *"Jalankan OCR dan simpan teks ke database"*

---
### **3.2 Endpoint & Metode Automation Rules**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/automation/rules`          | `POST` | Buat *rule* otomatisasi baru.             | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules"` |
| `/automation/rules`          | `GET`  | Daftar *rule* otomatisasi.                | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules"` |
| `/automation/rules/{ruleId}` | `GET`  | Ambil detail *rule*.                      | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules/rule_abc123"` |
| `/automation/rules/{ruleId}` | `PUT`  | Perbarui *rule*.                          | `curl -X PUT "https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules/rule_abc123"` |
| `/automation/rules/{ruleId}` | `DELETE`| Hapus *rule*.                             | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules/rule_abc123"` |

---
### **3.3 Buat Automation Rule (Create Rule)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `name`                  | string  | Nama *rule*.                                                                               | ✅     |
| `description`           | string  | Deskripsi *rule*.                                                                           | ❌     |
| `trigger`               | object  | *Trigger* untuk *rule* (misal: `asset.uploaded`, `conversation.created`).                     | ✅     |
| `action`                | object  | *Action* yang akan dijalankan (misal: `run_workflow`, `send_webhook`).                        | ✅     |
| `active`                | boolean | Apakah *rule* aktif.                                                                         | ❌     |

**Contoh `trigger` dan `action`:**
```json
{
  "trigger": {
    "type": "asset.uploaded",
    "conditions": {
      "assetType": "document"
    }
  },
  "action": {
    "type": "run_workflow",
    "workflowId": "wf_ocr_123"
  }
}
```

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "name": "OCR Otomatis untuk Dokumen",
  "description": "Jalankan workflow OCR saat dokumen diunggah.",
  "trigger": {
    "type": "asset.uploaded",
    "conditions": {
      "assetType": "document"
    }
  },
  "action": {
    "type": "run_workflow",
    "workflowId": "wf_ocr_123"
  },
  "active": true
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "ruleId": "rule_abc123",
  "name": "OCR Otomatis untuk Dokumen",
  "description": "Jalankan workflow OCR saat dokumen diunggah.",
  "trigger": {
    "type": "asset.uploaded",
    "conditions": {
      "assetType": "document"
    }
  },
  "action": {
    "type": "run_workflow",
    "workflowId": "wf_ocr_123"
  },
  "active": true,
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:10:00Z"
}
```

---
### **3.4 Daftar Automation Rules (List Rules)**
**Endpoint:**
```
GET https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules
```
**Parameter Query Opsional:**
| Parameter | Tipe    | Deskripsi                          | Contoh               |
|-----------|---------|------------------------------------|----------------------|
| `active`  | boolean | Filter by *active* status.         | `active=true`       |
| `limit`   | number  | Batasan jumlah *rule*.             | `limit=10`          |
| `page`    | number  | Halaman (untuk paginasi).          | `page=1`            |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules?active=true&limit=10' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "rules": [
    {
      "ruleId": "rule_abc123",
      "name": "OCR Otomatis untuk Dokumen",
      "description": "Jalankan workflow OCR saat dokumen diunggah.",
      "trigger": {
        "type": "asset.uploaded",
        "conditions": {
          "assetType": "document"
        }
      },
      "action": {
        "type": "run_workflow",
        "workflowId": "wf_ocr_123"
      },
      "active": true,
      "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:10:00Z"
    },
    {
      "ruleId": "rule_def456",
      "name": "Notifikasi Slack untuk Error",
      "description": "Kirim notifikasi ke Slack jika workflow gagal.",
      "trigger": {
        "type": "workflow.failed"
      },
      "action": {
        "type": "send_webhook",
        "webhookUrl": "https://hooks.slack.com/services/...",
        "payload": {
          "text": "Workflow {{workflowId}} gagal! Error: {{error}}"
        }
      },
      "active": true,
      "createdAt": "2026-05-18T10:30:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "total": 2,
  "page": 1,
  "limit": 10
}
```

---
### **3.5 Praktik Terbaik untuk Automation Rules**
1. **Definisikan Trigger yang Jelas:**
   - Gunakan *trigger* yang spesifik (misal: `asset.uploaded` dengan `assetType=document`).
2. **Uji Rule Sebelum Aktifkan:**
   - Uji *rule* dalam mode *draft* sebelum mengaktifkannya.
3. **Monitoring Rule:**
   - Pantau *execution log* untuk *rule* yang aktif.
4. **Batasi Akses:**
   - Pastikan *rule* hanya dapat dibuat/perbarui oleh pengguna yang berwenang.

---
---
---
## **⏰ SEKSI 4: SCHEDULED WORKFLOWS (JADWAL WORKFLOW)**
---

### **4.1 Konsep Scheduled Workflows**
**Scheduled Workflows** memungkinkan Anda untuk:
- **Menjalankan *workflow* pada jadwal tertentu** (misal: *setiap jam*, *setiap hari*, *setiap minggu*).
- **Menggunakan *cron expression*** untuk mendefinisikan jadwal.
- **Menangani *timezone*** untuk jadwal yang akurat.

**Contoh Jadwal:**
- **Setiap jam:** `0 * * * *` (menit ke-0 setiap jam).
- **Setiap hari pukul 09:00:** `0 9 * * *`.
- **Setiap Senin pukul 00:00:** `0 0 * * 1`.

---
### **4.2 Endpoint & Metode Scheduled Workflows**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/schedule` | `POST` | Jadwalkan *workflow*.                     | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/schedule"` |
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/schedule` | `GET`  | Ambil jadwal *workflow*.                  | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/schedule"` |
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/schedule` | `PUT`  | Perbarui jadwal *workflow*.               | `curl -X PUT "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/schedule"` |
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/schedule` | `DELETE`| Hapus jadwal *workflow*.                  | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/schedule"` |

---
### **4.3 Jadwalkan Workflow (Schedule Workflow)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflowId}/schedule
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `cronExpression`        | string  | *Cron expression* untuk jadwal.                                                             | ✅     |
| `timezone`              | string  | *Timezone* (misal: `Asia/Jakarta`).                                                          | ✅     |
| `input`                 | object  | *Input* untuk *workflow* (opsional, bisa di-*override* saat *run*).                          | ❌     |
| `active`                | boolean | Apakah jadwal aktif.                                                                         | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/schedule' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "cronExpression": "0 9 * * *",
  "timezone": "Asia/Jakarta",
  "input": {
    "reportType": "daily"
  },
  "active": true
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "workflowId": "wf_abc123",
  "scheduleId": "sch_abc123",
  "cronExpression": "0 9 * * *",
  "timezone": "Asia/Jakarta",
  "input": {
    "reportType": "daily"
  },
  "active": true,
  "nextRunAt": "2026-05-20T09:00:00+07:00",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:15:00Z"
}
```

---
### **4.4 Praktik Terbaik untuk Scheduled Workflows**
1. **Gunakan Cron Expression yang Benar:**
   - Uji *cron expression* menggunakan *cron validator* (misal: [crontab.guru](https://crontab.guru/)).
2. **Perhatikan Timezone:**
   - Pastikan *timezone* sesuai dengan kebutuhan (misal: `Asia/Jakarta` untuk WIB).
3. **Monitoring Jadwal:**
   - Pantau *nextRunAt* dan *lastRunAt* untuk memastikan *workflow* berjalan sesuai jadwal.
4. **Input Default:**
   - Berikan *input* default yang dapat di-*override* jika diperlukan.

---
---
---
## **🌐 SEKSI 5: WEBHOOK TRIGGERS (PEMICU EKSTERNAL)**
---

### **5.1 Konsep Webhook Triggers**
**Webhook Triggers** memungkinkan *workflow* untuk dipicu oleh:
- **Webhook dari Sistem Eksternal** (misal: GitHub, Slack, Stripe).
- **Event dari 1Min.AI** (misal: `asset.uploaded`, `task.completed`).
- **Custom Webhook** (misal: *webhook* dari aplikasi internal).

**Contoh:**
- **Trigger:** *Webhook* dari GitHub saat *push* ke *repository*.
- **Action:** *Jalankan workflow* untuk *deploy* model AI yang baru.

---
### **5.2 Endpoint & Metode Webhook Triggers**
| Endpoint                     | Metode | Deskripsi                                  | Contoh Permintaan                          |
|------------------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/triggers` | `POST` | Tambah *webhook trigger* ke *workflow*. | `curl -X POST "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/triggers"` |
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/triggers/{triggerId}` | `GET` | Ambil detail *webhook trigger*.         | `curl -X GET "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/triggers/trigger_abc123"` |
| `/workflows/{workflowId}/triggers/{triggerId}` | `DELETE` | Hapus *webhook trigger*.               | `curl -X DELETE "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/triggers/trigger_abc123"` |

---
### **5.3 Tambah Webhook Trigger (Add Trigger)**
**Endpoint:**
```
POST https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflowId}/triggers
```
**Parameter:**
| Parameter               | Tipe    | Deskripsi                                                                                     | Wajib? |
|-------------------------|---------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--------|
| `type`                  | string  | Tipe *trigger* (`webhook`).                                                                 | ✅     |
| `webhookUrl`            | string  | URL *webhook* yang akan memicu *workflow*.                                                 | ✅     |
| `secret`                | string  | *Secret key* untuk verifikasi *webhook* (opsional).                                         | ❌     |
| `method`                | string  | Metode HTTP (`POST`, `GET`, dll.).                                                          | ❌     |
| `payloadMapping`        | object  | *Mapping* dari *payload* webhook ke *input* workflow.                                        | ❌     |

**Contoh Permintaan (cURL):**
```bash
curl --location 'https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/wf_abc123/triggers' \
--header 'API-KEY: <api-key>' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
  "type": "webhook",
  "webhookUrl": "https://github.com/webhooks/...",
  "secret": "your_webhook_secret",
  "method": "POST",
  "payloadMapping": {
    "input.repository": "repository.full_name",
    "input.commitId": "head_commit.id"
  }
}'
```

**Contoh Tanggapan:**
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "workflowId": "wf_abc123",
  "triggerId": "trigger_abc123",
  "type": "webhook",
  "webhookUrl": "https://github.com/webhooks/...",
  "secret": "your_webhook_secret",
  "method": "POST",
  "payloadMapping": {
    "input.repository": "repository.full_name",
    "input.commitId": "head_commit.id"
  },
  "active": true,
  "createdAt": "2026-05-19T12:20:00Z"
}
```

---
### **5.4 Contoh Payload Mapping**
**Payload Webhook dari GitHub:**
```json
{
  "repository": {
    "full_name": "dhani-yuliawan/musgo-os"
  },
  "head_commit": {
    "id": "abc123"
  }
}
```
**Payload Mapping:**
```json
{
  "input.repository": "repository.full_name",
  "input.commitId": "head_commit.id"
}
```
**Hasil Input untuk Workflow:**
```json
{
  "input": {
    "repository": "dhani-yuliawan/musgo-os",
    "commitId": "abc123"
  }
}
```

---
### **5.5 Praktik Terbaik untuk Webhook Triggers**
1. **Verifikasi Webhook:**
   - Selalu verifikasi *signature* webhook untuk menghindari *spoofing*.
2. **Gunakan Secret Key:**
   - Gunakan *secret key* untuk memastikan webhook berasal dari sumber yang tepercaya.
3. **Payload Mapping yang Jelas:**
   - Definisikan *mapping* yang jelas dari *payload* webhook ke *input* workflow.
4. **Error Handling:**
   - Tangani kesalahan dalam *webhook* (misal: *retry* jika *workflow* gagal).

---
---
---
## **🔄 SEKSI 6: CONDITIONAL LOGIC (LOGIKA KONDISIONAL)**
---

### **6.1 Konsep Conditional Logic**
**Conditional Logic** memungkinkan Anda untuk:
- **Menambahkan *if-else* dalam *workflow***.
- **Menjalankan *step* tertentu berdasarkan kondisi**.
- **Menggunakan *loop* untuk iterasi**.

**Contoh:**
```
1. Jika file berupa PDF → Jalankan OCR
2. Jika file berupa gambar → Jalankan Image-to-Text
3. Jika file berupa audio → Jalankan Speech-to-Text
```

---
### **6.2 Conditional Steps dalam Workflow**
**Contoh Workflow dengan Conditional Logic:**
```json
{
  "name": "Pemrosesan File Otomatis",
  "steps": [
    {
      "id": "step_1",
      "name": "Cek Tipe File",
      "type": "CONDITIONAL",
      "conditions": [
        {
          "if": "{{input.fileType}} == 'pdf'",
          "then": "step_ocr"
        },
        {
          "if": "{{input.fileType}} == 'image'",
          "then": "step_image_to_text"
        },
        {
          "if": "{{input.fileType}} == 'audio'",
          "then": "step_speech_to_text"
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "step_ocr",
      "name": "OCR untuk PDF",
      "type": "TEXT_GENERATOR",
      "model": "gpt-4o",
      "input": {
        "prompt": "Ekstrak teks dari PDF: {{input.fileUrl}}"
      },
      "output": "extractedText"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_image_to_text",
      "name": "Image-to-Text",
      "type": "IMAGE_TO_PROMPT",
      "model": "blip-2",
      "input": {
        "imageUrl": "{{input.fileUrl}}"
      },
      "output": "imageDescription"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_speech_to_text",
      "name": "Speech-to-Text",
      "type": "SPEECH_TO_TEXT",
      "model": "whisper-1",
      "input": {
        "audioUrl": "{{input.fileUrl}}"
      },
      "output": "transcript"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_4",
      "name": "Simpan Hasil",
      "type": "CUSTOM",
      "action": "save_to_database",
      "input": {
        "text": "{{steps.step_ocr.output || steps.step_image_to_text.output || steps.step_speech_to_text.output}}"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

---
### **6.3 Loop dalam Workflow**
**Contoh Workflow dengan Loop:**
```json
{
  "name": "Generasi Gambar Massal",
  "steps": [
    {
      "id": "step_1",
      "name": "Inisialisasi",
      "type": "CUSTOM",
      "action": "set_variable",
      "input": {
        "prompts": ["prompt 1", "prompt 2", "prompt 3"],
        "images": []
      },
      "output": "initialization"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_2",
      "name": "Loop Generasi Gambar",
      "type": "LOOP",
      "items": "{{steps.step_1.output.prompts}}",
      "step": {
        "id": "loop_step",
        "name": "Generasi Gambar",
        "type": "IMAGE_GENERATOR",
        "model": "dalle-3",
        "input": {
          "prompt": "{{item}}"
        },
        "output": "imageUrl"
      },
      "output": "generatedImages"
    },
    {
      "id": "step_3",
      "name": "Simpan Hasil",
      "type": "CUSTOM",
      "action": "save_to_database",
      "input": {
        "images": "{{steps.step_2.output}}"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

---
### **6.4 Praktik Terbaik untuk Conditional Logic**
1. **Gunakan Kondisi yang Jelas:**
   - Definisikan kondisi yang mudah dipahami dan di-*debug*.
2. **Avoid Deep Nesting:**
   - Hindari *nested conditional* yang terlalu dalam (sulit untuk dipelihara).
3. **Uji Semua Path:**
   - Uji semua *path* kondisional (misal: *if*, *else if*, *else*).
4. **Gunakan Default Path:**
   - Selalu sertakan *default path* untuk menghindari *unhandled cases*.

---
---
---
## **🔌 SEKSI 7: INTEGRASI AI WORKFLOWS & AUTOMATION DENGAN MUSGO-OS 2IN1**
---
---

### **7.1 Arsitektur Integrasi**
**MusGo-OS 2in1** dapat mengintegrasikan **AI Workflows & Automation** untuk:
1. **Modul Workflow Engine:**
   - Jalankan *workflow* untuk pemrosesan data otomatis.
2. **Modul Automation:**
   - Atur *automation rules* untuk pemicu otomatis.
3. **Modul Scheduler:**
   - Jadwalkan *workflow* untuk tugas berkala.
4. **Modul Webhook Handler:**
   - Terima *webhook* dari sistem eksternal dan picu *workflow*.

**Contoh Arsitektur:**
```
[User/System] → [MusGo-OS 2in1] → (Workflow Engine) → [1Min.AI Workflows API] → (Execute Steps)
[External System] → (Webhook) → [MusGo-OS 2in1 Webhook Handler] → (Trigger Workflow)
[MusGo-OS 2in1] → (Scheduler) → [1Min.AI Scheduled Workflows] → (Run on Schedule)
```

---
### **7.2 Contoh Implementasi (Python + FastAPI)**
**1. Setup Endpoint untuk Workflow Engine:**
```python
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, HTTPException, BackgroundTasks
import requests
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI()
DB_FILE = "musgo_workflows.db"

# Setup database
conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workflows (
        workflow_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT,
        description TEXT,
        steps TEXT,
        trigger_type TEXT,
        trigger_config TEXT,
        active BOOLEAN,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at TIMESTAMP
    )
""")
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workflow_runs (
        run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        workflow_id TEXT,
        input TEXT,
        output TEXT,
        status TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        completed_at TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY (workflow_id) REFERENCES workflows(workflow_id)
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/workflows")
async def create_workflow(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    workflow_id = data.get("workflowId")
    name = data["name"]
    description = data.get("description", "")
    steps = data["steps"]
    trigger = data.get("trigger", {"type": "manual"})

    # 1. Buat workflow di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "name": name,
            "description": description,
            "steps": steps,
            "trigger": trigger
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to create workflow")

    workflow_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan workflow ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO workflows (workflow_id, name, description, steps, trigger_type, trigger_config, active, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), datetime('now'))",
        (
            workflow_data["workflowId"],
            name,
            description,
            str(steps),
            trigger.get("type"),
            str(trigger.get("config", {})),
            trigger.get("active", True)
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "workflowId": workflow_data["workflowId"]}

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/run")
async def run_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks):
    data = await request.json()
    input_data = data.get("input", {})

    # 1. Jalankan workflow di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflow_id}/run",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={"input": input_data}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to run workflow")

    run_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan run ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO workflow_runs (run_id, workflow_id, input, status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))",
        (run_data["runId"], workflow_id, str(input_data), run_data["status"])
    )
    conn.commit()

    # 3. Pantau status run di background
    background_tasks.add_task(monitor_workflow_run, workflow_id, run_data["runId"])

    return {"status": "success", "runId": run_data["runId"]}

async def monitor_workflow_run(workflow_id: str, run_id: str):
    while True:
        # Ambil status run dari 1Min.AI
        response = requests.get(
            f"https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflow_id}/runs/{run_id}",
            headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY"}
        )
        if response.status_code != 200:
            break

        run_data = response.json()
        status = run_data["status"]

        # Perbarui status di database
        cursor.execute(
            "UPDATE workflow_runs SET status = ?, output = ?, completed_at = ? WHERE run_id = ?",
            (status, str(run_data.get("output", {})), run_data.get("completedAt"), run_id)
        )
        conn.commit()

        if status in ["completed", "failed", "cancelled"]:
            break

        await asyncio.sleep(5)  # Cek setiap 5 detik

@app.get("/workflows/{workflow_id}/runs/{run_id}")
async def get_workflow_run(workflow_id: str, run_id: str):
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM workflow_runs WHERE run_id = ?", (run_id,))
    run = cursor.fetchone()
    if not run:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Run not found")

    return {
        "runId": run[0],
        "workflowId": run[1],
        "input": eval(run[2]),
        "output": eval(run[3]),
        "status": run[4],
        "createdAt": run[5],
        "completedAt": run[6]
    }
```

**2. Setup Endpoint untuk Automation Rules:**
```python
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS automation_rules (
        rule_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT,
        description TEXT,
        workflow_id TEXT,
        trigger_type TEXT,
        trigger_config TEXT,
        active BOOLEAN,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY (workflow_id) REFERENCES workflows(workflow_id)
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/automation/rules")
async def create_automation_rule(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    name = data["name"]
    description = data.get("description", "")
    workflow_id = data["workflowId"]
    trigger = data["trigger"]
    active = data.get("active", True)

    # 1. Buat rule di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        "https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "name": name,
            "description": description,
            "trigger": trigger,
            "action": {
                "type": "run_workflow",
                "workflowId": workflow_id
            },
            "active": active
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to create automation rule")

    rule_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan rule ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO automation_rules (rule_id, name, description, workflow_id, trigger_type, trigger_config, active, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), datetime('now'))",
        (
            rule_data["ruleId"],
            name,
            description,
            workflow_id,
            trigger.get("type"),
            str(trigger.get("conditions", {})),
            active
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "ruleId": rule_data["ruleId"]}

@app.post("/automation/rules/{rule_id}/toggle")
async def toggle_automation_rule(rule_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    active = data["active"]

    # 1. Perbarui rule di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.put(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/automation/rules/{rule_id}",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={"active": active}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to update automation rule")

    # 2. Perbarui rule di database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "UPDATE automation_rules SET active = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE rule_id = ?",
        (active, rule_id)
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "active": active}
```

**3. Setup Endpoint untuk Scheduled Workflows:**
```python
cursor.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scheduled_workflows (
        schedule_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        workflow_id TEXT,
        cron_expression TEXT,
        timezone TEXT,
        input TEXT,
        active BOOLEAN,
        next_run_at TIMESTAMP,
        created_at TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY (workflow_id) REFERENCES workflows(workflow_id)
    )
""")
conn.commit()

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/schedule")
async def schedule_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    cron_expression = data["cronExpression"]
    timezone = data["timezone"]
    input_data = data.get("input", {})
    active = data.get("active", True)

    # 1. Jadwalkan workflow di 1Min.AI
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflow_id}/schedule",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "cronExpression": cron_expression,
            "timezone": timezone,
            "input": input_data,
            "active": active
        }
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to schedule workflow")

    schedule_data = response.json()

    # 2. Simpan jadwal ke database lokal
    cursor.execute(
        "INSERT INTO scheduled_workflows (schedule_id, workflow_id, cron_expression, timezone, input, active, next_run_at, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), datetime('now'))",
        (
            schedule_data["scheduleId"],
            workflow_id,
            cron_expression,
            timezone,
            str(input_data),
            active,
            schedule_data["nextRunAt"]
        )
    )
    conn.commit()

    return {"status": "success", "scheduleId": schedule_data["scheduleId"]}

@app.get("/workflows/{workflow_id}/schedule")
async def get_workflow_schedule(workflow_id: str):
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM scheduled_workflows WHERE workflow_id = ?", (workflow_id,))
    schedules = []
    for row in cursor.fetchall():
        schedules.append({
            "scheduleId": row[0],
            "workflowId": row[1],
            "cronExpression": row[2],
            "timezone": row[3],
            "input": eval(row[4]),
            "active": bool(row[5]),
            "nextRunAt": row[6],
            "createdAt": row[7],
            "updatedAt": row[8]
        })
    return {"status": "success", "schedules": schedules}
```

**4. Setup Webhook Handler untuk External Triggers:**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException
import hmac
import hashlib

@app.post("/webhooks/external")
async def handle_external_webhook(request: Request, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks):
    # 1. Validasi signature (jika ada)
    body = await request.body()
    signature = request.headers.get("X-Hub-Signature-256")
    if signature:
        secret = "YOUR_WEBHOOK_SECRET"
        expected_signature = hmac.new(
            secret.encode(),
            body,
            hashlib.sha256
        ).hexdigest()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(f"sha256={expected_signature}", signature):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Invalid signature")

    # 2. Parse payload
    payload = await request.json()
    event_type = request.headers.get("X-GitHub-Event", "unknown")

    # 3. Cari rule yang sesuai dengan trigger
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM automation_rules WHERE trigger_type = ? AND active = 1", (f"webhook.{event_type}",))
    rules = cursor.fetchall()

    for rule in rules:
        trigger_config = eval(rule[5])
        if "webhookUrl" in trigger_config and trigger_config["webhookUrl"] == request.url:
            # 4. Jalankan workflow yang terkait
            background_tasks.add_task(
                run_workflow_by_rule,
                rule[0],  # rule_id
                rule[3],  # workflow_id
                payload
            )

    return {"status": "success"}

async def run_workflow_by_rule(rule_id: str, workflow_id: str, payload: dict):
    # 1. Ambil trigger config
    cursor.execute("SELECT trigger_config FROM automation_rules WHERE rule_id = ?", (rule_id,))
    trigger_config = eval(cursor.fetchone()[0])

    # 2. Mapping payload ke input workflow
    input_data = {}
    if "payloadMapping" in trigger_config:
        for key, value in trigger_config["payloadMapping"].items():
            # Contoh: "input.repository" -> "repository.full_name"
            if "." in value:
                keys = value.split(".")
                data = payload
                for k in keys:
                    data = data.get(k, {})
                input_data[key] = data
            else:
                input_data[key] = payload.get(value)

    # 3. Jalankan workflow
    response = requests.post(
        f"https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflow_id}/run",
        headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={"input": input_data}
    )
    if response.status_code != 200:
        print(f"Failed to run workflow {workflow_id}: {response.text}")
    else:
        run_data = response.json()
        print(f"Workflow {workflow_id} started with run ID: {run_data['runId']}")
```

### **7.3 Modul Keamanan Tambahan**
Untuk memastikan integrasi **secure-by-default** dan **zero-trust compliant**, tambahkan:

#### **1. Validasi Workflow Input**
- Validasi *input* yang dikirim ke *workflow* untuk menghindari *injection* atau *abuse*.

**Contoh:**
```python
def validate_workflow_input(input_data: dict, workflow_id: str) -> bool:
    # Ambil definisi workflow
    cursor.execute("SELECT steps FROM workflows WHERE workflow_id = ?", (workflow_id,))
    workflow = cursor.fetchone()
    if not workflow:
        return False

    steps = eval(workflow[0])

    # Validasi input berdasarkan steps
    for step in steps:
        if "input" in step:
            for key, value in step["input"].items():
                if key.startswith("{{input.") and key.endswith("}}"):
                    input_key = key[8:-2]  # Extract "input.xxx"
                    if input_key not in input_data:
                        return False

    return True
```

#### **2. Rate Limiting untuk Workflow Runs**
- Batasi jumlah *workflow runs* per pengguna.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/run")
@limiter.limit("50/hour")
async def run_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request):
    ...
```

#### **3. Audit Logging untuk Workflows**
- Catat semua aktivitas *workflow* (pembuatan, eksekusi, penghapusan).

**Contoh:**
```python
import logging

logging.basicConfig(filename='workflows.log', level=logging.INFO)

def log_workflow_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    logging.info(
        f"{datetime.now()} | User: {user_id} | Action: {action} | Details: {details}"
    )

@app.post("/workflows")
async def create_workflow(request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    user_id = data.get("userId")
    log_workflow_activity(user_id, "create_workflow", {"workflowId": data.get("workflowId")})
    ...
```

#### **4. Enkripsi Data Sensitif dalam Workflows**
- Enkripsi *input* atau *output* yang mengandung data sensitif.

**Contoh (Python + Fernet):**
```python
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

key = Fernet.generate_key()
cipher_suite = Fernet(key)

def encrypt_workflow_input(input_data: dict) -> dict:
    encrypted_input = {}
    for k, v in input_data.items():
        if isinstance(v, str):
            encrypted_input[k] = cipher_suite.encrypt(v.encode()).decode()
        else:
            encrypted_input[k] = v
    return encrypted_input

def decrypt_workflow_input(encrypted_input: dict) -> dict:
    decrypted_input = {}
    for k, v in encrypted_input.items():
        if isinstance(v, str):
            decrypted_input[k] = cipher_suite.decrypt(v.encode()).decode()
        else:
            decrypted_input[k] = v
    return decrypted_input
```

**3. Setup Webhook Handler di MusGo-OS 2in1:**
```python
@app.post("/webhooks/github")
async def handle_github_webhook(request: Request):
    # Validasi signature
    body = await request.body()
    signature = request.headers.get("X-Hub-Signature-256")
    if not verify_github_signature(body, signature, "YOUR_GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Invalid signature")

    # Parse payload
    payload = await request.json()
    event = request.headers.get("X-GitHub-Event")

    if event == "push":
        # Jalankan workflow
        response = requests.post(
            f"https://api.1min.ai/api/workflows/{workflow_id}/run",
            headers={"API-KEY": "YOUR_API_KEY", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
            json={
                "input": {
                    "repository": payload["repository"]["full_name"],
                    "commitId": payload["head_commit"]["id"]
                }
            }
        )
        if response.status_code == 200:
            return {"status": "success", "runId": response.json()["runId"]}
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Failed to run workflow")

    return {"status": "ignored"}

def verify_github_signature(body: bytes, signature: str, secret: str) -> bool:
    if not signature:
        return False
    hash_object = hmac.new(secret.encode(), body, hashlib.sha256)
    expected_signature = f"sha256={hash_object.hexdigest()}"
    return hmac.compare_digest(expected_signature, signature)
```

**4. Uji Webhook:**
- Lakukan *push* ke repository GitHub.
- **GitHub** akan mengirim *webhook* ke `https://musgo-os.example.com/webhooks/github`.
- **MusGo-OS 2in1** akan memvalidasi *signature* dan menjalankan *workflow*.

## **🔒 SEKSI 9: KEAMANAN & COMPLIANCE UNTUK AI WORKFLOWS & AUTOMATION**
---
---

### **9.1 Zero Trust untuk AI Workflows**
- **Verifikasi Semua Akses:**
  - Pastikan hanya pengguna yang berwenang yang dapat membuat, menjalankan, atau menghapus *workflow*.
- **Enkripsi Data Intermediate:**
  - Enkripsi *output* dari *step* yang mengandung data sensitif.

**Contoh Middleware (FastAPI):**
```python
from fastapi import Request, HTTPException
import jwt

SECRET_KEY = "your_secret_key"

async def verify_workflow_access(request: Request, workflow_id: str):
    token = request.headers.get("Authorization")
    if not token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing token")

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token.split(" ")[1], SECRET_KEY, algorithms=["HS256"])
        user_id = payload["user_id"]

        # Cek apakah pengguna memiliki akses ke workflow
        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM workflows WHERE workflow_id = ? AND (owner_id = ? OR shared_with LIKE ?)",
            (workflow_id, user_id, f"%{user_id}%")
        )
        if not cursor.fetchone():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Unauthorized")
        return user_id
    except:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token")

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/run")
async def run_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = await verify_workflow_access(request, workflow_id)
    ...
```

---
### **9.2 Replay Protection untuk Workflow Runs**
- **Gunakan Nonce:**
  - Tambahkan `nonce` untuk setiap permintaan *workflow run*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import secrets

def generate_nonce() -> str:
    return secrets.token_hex(16)

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/run")
async def run_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    nonce = data.get("nonce")
    if not nonce or not validate_nonce(nonce):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid nonce")
    ...
```

---
### **9.3 Immutable Logging untuk Workflows**
- **Simpan Semua Aktivitas ke IPFS:**
  - Gunakan **IPFS** untuk *tamper-proof logging* aktivitas *workflow*.

**Contoh:**
```python
import ipfshttpclient

client = ipfshttpclient.connect()

def log_workflow_activity(user_id: str, action: str, details: dict):
    log_entry = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "user_id": user_id,
        "action": action,
        "details": details
    }
    log_hash = client.add_json(log_entry)
    return f"ipfs://{log_hash}"
```

---
### **9.4 Compliance (GDPR, PDP)**
- **Hapus Data Lama:**
  - Hapus *workflow runs* yang sudah tidak diperlukan (misal: > 30 hari).
- **Anonimisasi Data:**
  - Anonimisasi data sensitif dalam *workflow input/output*.

**Contoh:**
```python
def anonymize_workflow_data(data: dict) -> dict:
    if "userId" in data:
        data["userId"] = "*****"
    if "email" in data:
        data["email"] = "*****@*****.com"
    return data
```

---
### **9.5 Workflow Input/Output Validation**
- **Validasi Input Workflow:**
  - Pastikan *input* yang dikirim ke *workflow* sesuai dengan skema yang diharapkan.

**Contoh:**
```python
from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError

class WorkflowInput(BaseModel):
    fileUrl: str
    fileType: str

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/run")
async def run_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request):
    data = await request.json()
    try:
        input_data = WorkflowInput(**data["input"])
    except ValidationError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Invalid input: {e}")
    ...
```

---
### **9.6 Rate Limiting untuk Workflow Runs**
- **Batasi Jumlah Workflow Runs:**
  - Batasi jumlah *workflow runs* per pengguna untuk menghindari *abuse*.

**Contoh (FastAPI + SlowAPI):**
```python
from slowapi import Limiter
from slowapi.util import get_remote_address

limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
app.state.limiter = limiter

@app.post("/workflows/{workflow_id}/run")
@limiter.limit("100/hour")
async def run_workflow(workflow_id: str, request: Request):
    ...
```

## **📌 END OF PART 10**
---
---
### **🔹 Ringkasan Part 10**
- **AI Workflows:**
  - Rantai **beberapa fitur AI** menjadi alur kerja otomatis.
  - Endpoint: `/workflows`, `/workflows/{workflowId}/run`, `/workflows/{workflowId}/runs`.
  - Fitur: *Sequential Steps*, *Parallel Steps*, *Conditional Logic*, *Loop*.
  - Parameter: `name`, `steps`, `trigger`, `input`.
- **Automation Rules:**
  - Definisikan *trigger* dan *action* untuk otomatisasi tugas.
  - Endpoint: `/automation/rules`, `/automation/rules/{ruleId}`.
  - Fitur: *Asset Uploaded*, *Conversation Created*, *Workflow Failed*, *Custom Webhook*.
- **Scheduled Workflows:**
  - Jalankan *workflow* pada jadwal tertentu menggunakan *cron expression*.
  - Endpoint: `/workflows/{workflowId}/schedule`.
- **Webhook Triggers:**
  - Pemicu *workflow* berdasarkan *webhook* dari sistem eksternal.
  - Endpoint: `/workflows/{workflowId}/triggers`.
  - Fitur: *Payload Mapping*, *Secret Verification*.
- **Conditional Logic:**
  - Tambahkan *if-else* dan *loop* dalam *workflow*.
  - Contoh: *Conditional Steps*, *Loop Steps*.
- **Integrasi dengan MusGo-OS 2in1:**
  - Arsitektur: Workflow Engine → Automation Service → Scheduler → Webhook Handler.
  - Contoh implementasi: FastAPI + SQLite + Background Tasks.
- **Contoh Alur Kerja:**
  - Pemrosesan dokumen otomatis (PDF → Teks → Terjemahan → Ringkasan).
  - Generasi konten massal (100 gambar → Unggah ke CDN).
  - Otomatisasi dengan *webhook trigger* (GitHub *push* → *Deploy* model).
  - *Scheduled workflow* (Ringkasan harian → Kirim email).
- **Keamanan & Compliance:**
  - **Zero Trust** (verifikasi akses, enkripsi data).
  - **Replay Protection** (nonce).
  - **Immutable Logging** (IPFS/blockchain).
  - **Compliance** (GDPR, PDP: hapus data lama, anonimisasi).
  - **Input/Output Validation** (validasi skema input).
  - **Rate Limiting** (batasi *workflow runs*).
